                </div>
                <div id="adminRowsPreview"
                  style="max-height:260px; overflow:auto; padding:10px 12px; font-family:var(--mono); font-size:12px;">
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

            <!-- HELP TAB -->
            <div id="adminTabHelp" style="display:none; color:var(--muted); line-height:1.6;">
              <ul class="feature-list" style="margin-top:6px;">
                <li><b>Edit question:</b> Select <code>CPOS_Advanced_Q_Bank</code> → enter <code>Question_ID</code> →
                  <b>Fetch Row</b> → edit → <b>Save Row</b>.
                </li>
                <li><b>Add question:</b> click <b>New Row</b> → fill fields (leave <code>Question_ID</code> blank) →
                  <b>Save Row</b> → copy generated ID from status.
                </li>
                <li><b>Linked tables:</b> paste that <code>Question_ID</code> into Mapping Editor →
                  <b>Load All Linked Sheets</b> → add rows → <b>Save All Linked Sheets</b>.
                </li>
                <li><b>Delete:</b> This editor uses <b>soft delete</b>. Set <code>Is_Active = FALSE</code> in Q_Bank
                  and/or the linked tables you want disabled.</li>
                <li><b>Safety:</b> Always save Q_Bank first (so ID exists), then create Options / Rules / Maps using
                  that ID.</li>
              </ul>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <script>
    // MULTI-MODULE SPA NAVIGATION
        let currentModule = 'csop';
        let sidebarCollapsed = false;

        function switchModule(moduleName) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.module-container').forEach(m => {
                m.classList.remove('active');
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => {
                n.classList.remove('active');
            });

            const moduleMap = {
                'client': 'clientModule',
                'csop': 'csopModule',
                'queries': 'queriesModule',
                'admin': 'adminModule',
                'advanced': 'advancedModule'
            };

            if (!moduleMap[moduleName]) {
                console.error('[Module] Unknown module:', moduleName);
                return;
            }

            var moduleElement = document.getElementById(moduleMap[moduleName]);
            if (moduleElement) {
                moduleElement.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                console.error('[Module] Module element not found:', moduleMap[moduleName]);
            }

            var navElement = document.getElementById('nav-' + moduleName) || document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-module="' + moduleName + '"]');
            if (navElement) {
                navElement.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                console.error('[Module] Nav element not found: nav-' + moduleName);
            }

            currentModule = moduleName;

            if (moduleName === 'admin' && typeof initAdminModule === 'function') {
                initAdminModule();
            }


            console.log('[Module] Switched to:', moduleName);

        }

        window.switchModule = switchModule;

        function toggleSidebar() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const toggleIcon = document.getElementById('toggleIcon');

            sidebar.classList.toggle('collapsed');
            sidebarCollapsed = !sidebarCollapsed;

            toggleIcon.textContent = sidebarCollapsed ? '▶' : '◀';

            console.log('[Sidebar] Collapsed:', sidebarCollapsed);
        }


        window.toggleSidebar = toggleSidebar;

        // Mobile menu toggle
        function toggleMobileMenu() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const isOpen = sidebar.classList.toggle('mobile-open');
            const btn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
            if (btn) btn.textContent = isOpen ? '✕' : '☰';
        }

        window.toggleMobileMenu = toggleMobileMenu;

        window.AdminQE = {
            initialized: false,
            selectedQid: '',
            selectedResult: null,
            packageData: null,
            activeTab: 'options',
            tableHeaders: {},
            dimValues: []
        };

        function qeSetStatus(msg, isError) {
            var el = document.getElementById('qeStatusBox');
            if (!el) return;
            el.style.color = isError ? '#b42318' : 'var(--muted)';
            el.textContent = msg || '';
        }

        function qeSetValidation(items) {
            var el = document.getElementById('qeValidationSummary');
            if (!el) return;
            if (!items || !items.length) {
                el.innerHTML = '';
                return;
            }
            el.innerHTML = items.map(function(x) {
                return '<div>• ' + adminEscapeHtml(x) + '</div>';
            }).join('');
        }

        function qeGetSchema(table) {
            return (window.CPOS_ADV_SCHEMA.tables || {})[table] || {};
        }

        function qeRenderSearchResults(rows) {
            var host = document.getElementById('qeSearchResults');
            if (!host) return;
            var list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
            if (!list.length) {
                host.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:10px;color:var(--muted);">No questions found.</div>';
                return;
            }
            host.innerHTML = list.map(function(r) {
                var q = adminEscapeHtml(r.Question_ID || '');
                var active = String(r.Is_Active || '').toUpperCase() === 'FALSE' ? 'Inactive' : 'Active';
                var st = active === 'Active' ? '#1f5f2f' : '#7a3f0a';
                return '<div class="qe-result" data-qid="' + q + '" style="padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--stroke2);cursor:pointer;">' +
                    '<div style="font-weight:700;">' + q + ' <span style="font-size:11px;color:' + st + ';">' + active + '</span></div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);">' + adminEscapeHtml((r.Dimension_ID || '') + ' • ' + (r.Sub_Factor || '') + ' • Seq ' + (r.Sequence || '')) + '</div><div style="font-size:12px;">' + adminEscapeHtml(String(r.Question_Text_EN || '').slice(0, 120)) + '</div></div>';
            }).join('');
            host.querySelectorAll('.qe-result').forEach(function(el) {
                el.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    window.AdminQE.selectedQid = el.getAttribute('data-qid') || '';
                    host.querySelectorAll('.qe-result').forEach(function(x) {
                        x.style.background = '';
                    });
                    el.style.background = 'rgba(47,111,181,0.08)';
                });
            });
        }

        function qeSearch() {
            var q = ((document.getElementById('qeSearch') || {}).value || '').trim();
            adminGsRun('cpos_adminSearchQuestions', q, 100).then(function(resp) {
                qeRenderSearchResults(resp.rows || []);
                qeSetStatus('Search complete: ' + (resp.rows || []).length + ' result(s).', false);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Search failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function qeRenderBankForm(bankRow) {
            var host = document.getElementById('qeBankForm');
            if (!host) return;
            var headers = window.AdminQE.tableHeaders['CPOS_Advanced_Q_Bank'] || Object.keys(bankRow || {});
            var schema = qeGetSchema('CPOS_Advanced_Q_Bank');
            host.innerHTML = '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:10px;">' + headers.map(function(h) {
                var v = (bankRow && bankRow[h] != null) ? bankRow[h] : '';
                var bool = (schema.boolCols || []).indexOf(h) >= 0;
                var num = (schema.numCols || []).indexOf(h) >= 0;
                var ta = (schema.textAreaCols || []).indexOf(h) >= 0;
                var ro = (h === 'Question_ID' && v);
                var field = '';
                if (h === 'Dimension_ID') {
                    field = '<select data-qe-bank="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" style="width:100%;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;">' + window.AdminQE.dimValues.map(function(d) {
                        var s = String(d) === String(v) ? 'selected' : '';
                        return '<option ' + s + '>' + adminEscapeHtml(d) + '</option>';
                    }).join('') + '</select>';
                } else if (bool) {
                    var sv = String(v || 'TRUE').toUpperCase() === 'FALSE' ? 'FALSE' : 'TRUE';
                    field = '<select data-qe-bank="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" style="width:100%;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;"><option ' + (sv === 'TRUE' ? 'selected' : '') + '>TRUE</option><option ' + (sv === 'FALSE' ? 'selected' : '') + '>FALSE</option></select>';
                } else if (h === 'Answer_Type') {
                    var opts = ['single_choice', 'multi_select', 'numeric', 'text', 'yes_no_conditional', 'categorical_direct'];
                    field = '<select data-qe-bank="Answer_Type" style="width:100%;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;">' + opts.map(function(o) {
                        return '<option ' + (String(v) === o ? 'selected' : '') + '>' + o + '</option>';
                    }).join('') + '</select>';
                } else if (ta) {
                    field = '<textarea data-qe-bank="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" rows="2" style="width:100%;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;" ' + (ro ? 'readonly' : '') + '>' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '</textarea>';
                } else {
                    field = '<input data-qe-bank="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" type="' + (num ? 'number' : 'text') + '" value="' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '" style="width:100%;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;" ' + (ro ? 'readonly' : '') + ' />';
                }
                return '<div><label style="display:block;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:4px;">' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '</label>' + field + '</div>';
            }).join('') + '</div>';
            adminBindAutosize(host);
        }

        function qeCollectBankRow() {
            var row = {};
            (document.querySelectorAll('[data-qe-bank]') || []).forEach(function(el) {
                row[el.getAttribute('data-qe-bank')] = el.value;
            });
            return row;
        }

        function qeRenderRowsTable(key, rows, headers, schemaTable) {
            var schema = qeGetSchema(schemaTable);
            var h = Array.isArray(headers) && headers.length ? headers : (rows[0] ? Object.keys(rows[0]) : []);
            if (!h.length) return '<div style="color:var(--muted);">No headers.</div>';
            var body = (rows || [{}]).map(function(r, idx) {
                return '<tr><td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' + h.map(function(col) {
                    var v = (r && r[col] != null) ? r[col] : '';
                    var bool = (schema.boolCols || []).indexOf(col) >= 0;
                    var num = (schema.numCols || []).indexOf(col) >= 0;
                    var csv = (schema.csvCols || []).indexOf(col) >= 0;
                    var ta = (schema.textAreaCols || []).indexOf(col) >= 0;
                    var token = (schema.tokenFields || []).indexOf(col) >= 0;
                    var input = '';
                    if (bool) {
                        var sv = String(v || 'TRUE').toUpperCase() === 'FALSE' ? 'FALSE' : 'TRUE';
                        input = '<select data-qe-row="' + key + '" data-row="' + idx + '" data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(col) + '"><option ' + (sv === 'TRUE' ? 'selected' : '') + '>TRUE</option><option ' + (sv === 'FALSE' ? 'selected' : '') + '>FALSE</option></select>';
                    } else if (ta) {
                        input = '<textarea data-qe-row="' + key + '" data-row="' + idx + '" data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(col) + '" rows="2">' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '</textarea>';
                    } else if (num) {
                        input = '<input data-qe-row="' + key + '" data-row="' + idx + '" data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(col) + '" type="number" value="' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '" />';
                    } else {
                        input = '<input data-qe-row="' + key + '" data-row="' + idx + '" data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(col) + '" type="text" value="' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '" />';
                    }
                    var helper = (csv ? '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);">CSV</div>' : '') + (token ? '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);">Token field</div>' : '');
                    return '<td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);min-width:220px;">' + input + helper + '</td>';
                }).join('') + '<td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-qe-remove="' + key + '" data-row="' + idx + '">Remove</button></td></tr>';
            }).join('');
            return '<div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-qe-add="' + key + '">Add Row</button></div><div style="overflow:auto;"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:max-content;min-width:100%;"><thead><tr><th style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);">#</th>' + h.map(function(col) {
                return '<th style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);">' + adminEscapeHtml(col) + '</th>';
            }).join('') + '<th style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);">Action</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + body + '</tbody></table></div>';
        }

        function qeRenderActiveTab() {
            var body = document.getElementById('qeTabBody');
            if (!body) return;
            var pkg = (window.AdminQE.packageData || {});
            var tab = window.AdminQE.activeTab || 'options';
            var map = {
                options: ['options', 'CPOS_Advanced_Q_Options'],
                routing: ['routingOut', 'CPOS_Advanced_Routing_Rules'],
                derivation: ['derivation', 'CPOS_Advanced_Derivation_Rules'],
                summary: ['summary', 'CPOS_Advanced_Summary_Checkpoints'],
                inference: ['inference', 'CPOS_Advanced_Inference_Rules'],
                composite: ['composite', 'CPOS_Advanced_Composite_Rules'],
                signalmap: ['signalMap', 'CPOS_Advanced_Signal_to_LiteFactor_Map'],
                probes: ['probes', 'CPOS_Advanced_Probe_Templates']
            };
            if (tab === 'deps') {
                var d = pkg.dependencySummary || {};
                body.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);">Inbound refs: ' + adminEscapeHtml(JSON.stringify(d.inbound || [])) + '</div>';
                return;
            }
            if (tab === 'preview') {
                body.innerHTML = '<pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:12px;">' + adminEscapeHtml(JSON.stringify(pkg, null, 2)) + '</pre>';
                return;
            }
            if (tab === 'routing') {
                var ro = map[tab];
                var t = qeRenderRowsTable(tab, pkg[ro[0]] || [], window.AdminQE.tableHeaders[ro[1]] || [], ro[1]);
                var inbound = pkg.routingIn || [];
                body.innerHTML = t + '<div style="margin-top:10px;font-weight:700;">Inbound (read-only)</div><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:12px;">' + adminEscapeHtml(JSON.stringify(inbound, null, 2)) + '</pre>';
            } else {
                var meta = map[tab];
                if (!meta) {
                    body.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);">Not applicable for this package.</div>';
                    return;
                }
                body.innerHTML = qeRenderRowsTable(tab, pkg[meta[0]] || [], window.AdminQE.tableHeaders[meta[1]] || [], meta[1]);
            }
            body.querySelectorAll('[data-qe-add]').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.onclick = function() {
                    var k = btn.getAttribute('data-qe-add');
                    var arr = window.AdminQE.packageData[k === 'routing' ? 'routingOut' : k] || [];
                    arr.push({
                        Question_ID: window.AdminQE.selectedQid,
                        From_Question_ID: window.AdminQE.selectedQid,
                        When_Question_ID: window.AdminQE.selectedQid,
                        Trigger_After_Question: window.AdminQE.selectedQid
                    });
                    window.AdminQE.packageData[k === 'routing' ? 'routingOut' : k] = arr;
                    qeRenderActiveTab();
                };
            });
            body.querySelectorAll('[data-qe-remove]').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.onclick = function() {
                    var k = btn.getAttribute('data-qe-remove');
                    var idx = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-row'));
                    var key = (k === 'routing' ? 'routingOut' : k);
                    var arr = window.AdminQE.packageData[key] || [];
                    arr.splice(idx, 1);
                    window.AdminQE.packageData[key] = arr;
                    qeRenderActiveTab();
                };
            });
            adminBindAutosize(body);
        }

        function qeCollectRows(tabKey) {
            var out = [];
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-qe-row="' + tabKey + '"]').forEach(function(el) {
                var r = Number(el.getAttribute('data-row'));
                var c = el.getAttribute('data-col');
                out[r] = out[r] || {};
                out[r][c] = el.value;
            });
            return out.filter(Boolean);
        }

        function qeLoadQuestion(qid) {
            var id = String(qid || window.AdminQE.selectedQid || '').trim();
            if (!id) {
                qeSetStatus('Select Question_ID first.', true);
                return;
            }
            adminGsRun('cpos_getQuestionPackage', id).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) throw new Error('load failed');
                window.AdminQE.packageData = resp.pkg || {};
                window.AdminQE.selectedQid = id;
                document.getElementById('qeHeaderInfo').textContent = 'Question: ' + id + ' • ' + ((resp.pkg.bankRow || {}).Is_Active === 'FALSE' ? 'Inactive' : 'Active');
                qeRenderBankForm(resp.pkg.bankRow || {});
                qeRenderActiveTab();
                qeSetStatus('Package loaded.', false);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Load failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function qeValidateBeforeSave() {
            var w = [];
            var bank = qeCollectBankRow();
            if (!String(bank.Question_ID || '').trim()) w.push('Question_ID is required.');
            var ans = String(bank.Answer_Type || '').trim();
            var options = qeCollectRows('options');
            if ((ans === 'categorical_direct' || ans === 'yes_no_conditional') && options.some(function(r) {
                    return !String(r.Option_Code || '').trim();
                })) w.push('Option_Code missing in options for categorical answer type.');
            var vr = String(bank.Validation_Rule || '');
            if (vr && /\{[^}]+\}/.test(vr) && !/ADV_/i.test(vr)) w.push('Validation_Rule may contain unknown variables.');
            return w;
        }

        function qeSavePackage() {
            if (!window.AdminQE.packageData) {
                qeSetStatus('Load a package first.', true);
                return;
            }
            var warnings = qeValidateBeforeSave();
            qeSetValidation(warnings);
            if (warnings.some(function(x) {
                    return x.indexOf('required') >= 0;
                })) {
                qeSetStatus('Fix validation errors before save.', true);
                return;
            }
            var pkg = {
                questionId: window.AdminQE.selectedQid,
                bankRow: qeCollectBankRow(),
                options: qeCollectRows('options'),
                routingOut: qeCollectRows('routing'),
                derivation: qeCollectRows('derivation'),
                summary: qeCollectRows('summary')
            };
            adminGsRun('cpos_saveQuestionPackage', pkg).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) throw new Error('save failed');
                qeSetStatus('Saved. Created: ' + (resp.counts.created || 0) + ', Updated: ' + (resp.counts.updated || 0) + ', Deactivated: ' + (resp.counts.deactivated || 0), false);
                qeLoadQuestion(resp.questionId || window.AdminQE.selectedQid);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Save failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function qeDeactivate() {
            var qid = window.AdminQE.selectedQid;
            if (!qid) {
                qeSetStatus('Load a package first.', true);
                return;
            }
            if (!confirm('Deactivate package ' + qid + ' ?')) return;
            adminGsRun('cpos_deactivateQuestionPackage', qid, 'minimal').then(function(resp) {
                qeSetStatus('Deactivated. Warnings: ' + ((resp.warnings || []).length), false);
                qeLoadQuestion(qid);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Deactivate failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function qeDuplicate() {
            var bank = qeCollectBankRow();
            if (!bank.Question_ID) {
                qeSetStatus('Load package first.', true);
                return;
            }
            var copy = Object.assign({}, bank);
            delete copy.Question_ID;
            adminGsRun('cpos_createNewQuestionWizard', {
                Dimension_ID: copy.Dimension_ID,
                Sub_Factor: copy.Sub_Factor,
                Answer_Type: copy.Answer_Type,
                Unit: copy.Unit,
                Question_Text_EN: copy.Question_Text_EN,
                Question_Text_HI: copy.Question_Text_HI,
                Help_Text_EN: copy.Help_Text_EN,
                Help_Text_HI: copy.Help_Text_HI
            }).then(function(resp) {
                qeSetStatus('Duplicated to ' + resp.questionId, false);
                qeLoadQuestion(resp.questionId);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Duplicate failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function qeNewWizard() {
            var dim = prompt('Dimension_ID (e.g., D1):', 'D1');
            if (!dim) return;
            var sub = prompt('Sub_Factor:', 'GENERAL') || '';
            var at = prompt('Answer_Type:', 'single_choice') || 'single_choice';
            var qt = prompt('Question_Text_EN:', '');
            adminGsRun('cpos_createNewQuestionWizard', {
                Dimension_ID: dim,
                Sub_Factor: sub,
                Answer_Type: at,
                Question_Text_EN: qt
            }).then(function(resp) {
                qeSetStatus('Created ' + resp.questionId, false);
                qeLoadQuestion(resp.questionId);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                qeSetStatus('Create failed: ' + (err.message || err), true);
            });
        }

        function initAdminQuestionEditor() {
            if (window.AdminQE.initialized) return;
            var q = document.getElementById('qeSearch');
            if (q) q.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    qeSearch();
                }
            });
            var btns = {
                qeSearchBtn: qeSearch,
                qeLoadBtn: function() {
                    qeLoadQuestion();
                },
                qeSavePkgBtn: qeSavePackage,
                qeDeactivateBtn: qeDeactivate,
                qeDuplicateBtn: qeDuplicate,
                qeNewBtn: qeNewWizard
            };
            Object.keys(btns).forEach(function(id) {
                var el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (el) el.addEventListener('click', btns[id]);
            });
            [
                ['qeTabOptionsBtn', 'options'],
                ['qeTabRoutingBtn', 'routing'],
                ['qeTabDerivationBtn', 'derivation'],
                ['qeTabInferenceBtn', 'inference'],
                ['qeTabCompositeBtn', 'composite'],
                ['qeTabSignalMapBtn', 'signalmap'],
                ['qeTabProbesBtn', 'probes'],
                ['qeTabSummaryBtn', 'summary'],
                ['qeTabDepsBtn', 'deps'],
                ['qeTabPreviewBtn', 'preview']
            ].forEach(function(x) {
                var el = document.getElementById(x[0]);
                if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    window.AdminQE.activeTab = x[1];
                    qeRenderActiveTab();
                });
            });
            adminGsRun('cpos_getAdvancedAdminConfig').then(function(resp) {
                (resp.tables || []).forEach(function(t) {
                    window.AdminQE.tableHeaders[t.name] = t.headers || [];
                    if (t.name === 'CPOS_Advanced_Q_Bank') {
                        var i = (t.headers || []).indexOf('Dimension_ID');
                        window.AdminQE.dimValues = (t.firstRows || []).map(function(r) {
                            return r['Dimension_ID'];
                        }).filter(Boolean).filter(function(v, i, a) {
                            return a.indexOf(v) === i;
                        });
                    }
                });
            }).catch(function() {});
            window.AdminQE.initialized = true;
        }

        function adminGsRun(fnName) {
            var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
            return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
                if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
                    reject(new Error('Apps Script runtime not available'));
                    return;
                }
                var runner = google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(resolve).withFailureHandler(reject);
                if (typeof runner[fnName] !== 'function') {
                    reject(new Error('Function not found: ' + fnName));
                    return;
                }
                runner[fnName].apply(runner, args);
            });
        }

        function adminSetStatus(msg, isError) {
            var el = document.getElementById('adminStatusBox');
            if (!el) return;
            el.style.color = isError ? '#b42318' : 'var(--text)';
            el.textContent = msg || '';
        }

        function adminSetMappingStatus(msg, isError) {
            var el = document.getElementById('adminMappingStatus');
            if (!el) return;
            el.style.color = isError ? '#b42318' : 'var(--muted)';
            el.textContent = msg || '';
        }

        function adminEscapeHtml(s) {
            return String(s == null ? '' : s)
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
        }

        function adminGetCurrentIdColumn() {
            var cfg = window.AdminEditor.currentConfig;
            return cfg && cfg.idColumn ? cfg.idColumn : '';
        }


        function adminSupportsQuestionMappings() {
            var list = window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs || [];
            return Array.isArray(list) && list.length > 0;
        }

        function adminToggleMappingEditor() {
            var panel = document.getElementById('adminMappingEditor');
            if (!panel) return;
            var show = adminSupportsQuestionMappings();
            panel.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
            if (!show) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('No linked Question_ID mapping sheets available.', false);
            } else {
                adminSetMappingStatus('Load and edit linked rows across all mapping sheets for one Question_ID.', false);
            }
        }


        function adminAutoResizeTextarea(el) {
            if (!el) return;
            el.style.height = 'auto';
            el.style.height = Math.max(56, el.scrollHeight) + 'px';
        }

        function adminBindAutosize(scopeEl) {
            if (!scopeEl) return;
            scopeEl.querySelectorAll('textarea').forEach(function(el) {
                adminAutoResizeTextarea(el);
                el.addEventListener('input', function() {
                    adminAutoResizeTextarea(el);
                });
            });
        }

        function adminRenderForm(headers, row) {
            var form = document.getElementById('adminDynamicForm');
            if (!form) return;
            if (!headers || !headers.length) {
                form.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);">No editable columns found.</div>';
                return;
            }

            var idCol = adminGetCurrentIdColumn();
            var thead = '<thead><tr>' + headers.map(function(h) {
                var autoTag = (idCol && h === idCol) ? ' (auto)' : '';
                return '<th style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--surface2);z-index:1;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);text-align:left;white-space:nowrap;">' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + autoTag + '</th>';
            }).join('') + '</tr></thead>';

            var tbody = '<tbody><tr>' + headers.map(function(h) {
                return '<td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);vertical-align:top;min-width:220px;">' +
                    '<textarea data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" rows="2" style="width:100%;resize:none;overflow:hidden;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;background:var(--surface);font-family:var(--sans);"></textarea>' +
                    '</td>';
            }).join('') + '</tr></tbody>';

            form.innerHTML = '<div style="overflow:auto;max-height:420px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:10px;background:var(--surface2);">' +
                '<table style="border-collapse:collapse;min-width:max-content;width:max-content;">' + thead + tbody + '</table>' +
                '</div>';
            adminFillForm(row || {});
        }

        function adminFillForm(row) {
            var form = document.getElementById('adminDynamicForm');
            if (!form) return;
            form.querySelectorAll('textarea[data-col]').forEach(function(el) {
                var key = el.getAttribute('data-col');
                var val = (row && row[key] !== undefined && row[key] !== null) ? row[key] : '';
                el.value = String(val);
            });

            var idCol = adminGetCurrentIdColumn();
            if (idCol) {
                var idField = form.querySelector('textarea[data-col="' + idCol.replace(/"/g, '\"') + '"]');
                if (idField && !idField.value) {
                    idField.placeholder = '(leave blank to auto-generate)';
                }
            }

            adminBindAutosize(form);
        }

        function adminCollectFormRow() {
            var out = {};
            var form = document.getElementById('adminDynamicForm');
            if (!form) return out;
            form.querySelectorAll('textarea[data-col]').forEach(function(el) {
                var key = el.getAttribute('data-col');
                out[key] = el.value;
            });
            return out;
        }

        function adminRenderPreview(rows, headers) {
            var el = document.getElementById('adminRowsPreview');
            if (!el) return;
            if (!rows || !rows.length) {
                el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--muted);">No rows found.</span>';
                return;
            }

            var useRows = rows.slice(0, 30);
            var html = useRows.map(function(r, idx) {
                var kv = (headers || Object.keys(r)).slice(0, 6).map(function(h) {
                    return '<div><strong>' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + ':</strong> ' + adminEscapeHtml(r[h]) + '</div>';
                }).join('');
                return '<div style="padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--stroke2);">' +
                    '<div style="color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:4px;">#' + (idx + 1) + '</div>' + kv + '</div>';
            }).join('');
            el.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function adminSetInfo(cfg) {
            var info = document.getElementById('adminInfoBox');
            if (!info) return;
            if (!cfg) {
                info.textContent = '';
                return;
            }
            info.innerHTML = 'Table: <strong>' + adminEscapeHtml(cfg.name) + '</strong>' +
                (cfg.idColumn ? (' • ID: <strong>' + adminEscapeHtml(cfg.idColumn) + '</strong>') : ' • ID lookup: not configured');
        }


        function adminRenderMappingRowsTable_(cfg, rows) {
            var headers = cfg.headers || [];
            var sheetName = cfg.sheetName;
            var host = document.getElementById('adminMappingRows');
            if (!host) return;

            var wrap = document.createElement('div');
            wrap.style.border = '1px solid var(--stroke)';
            wrap.style.borderRadius = '10px';
            wrap.style.background = 'var(--surface)';
            wrap.style.padding = '10px';
            wrap.style.minWidth = '0';
            wrap.style.width = '100%';

            var title = '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:8px;">' +
                '<div><strong>' + adminEscapeHtml(sheetName) + '</strong> <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;">(link: ' + adminEscapeHtml(cfg.linkColumn || '') + ')</span></div>' +
                '<button type="button" class="btn ghost adminAddMapRowBtn" data-sheet-name="' + adminEscapeHtml(sheetName) + '" style="padding:6px 10px;">Add Row</button>' +
                '</div>';

            if (!headers.length) {
                wrap.innerHTML = title + '<div style="color:var(--muted);">No headers found for this sheet.</div>';
                host.appendChild(wrap);

                return;
            }

            if (!rows || !rows.length) rows = [{}];

            var cellMinWidth = 320;
            var tableMinWidth = Math.max(1600, (headers.length * cellMinWidth) + 64 + 110);


            var thead = '<thead><tr>' +
                '<th style="position:sticky;top:0;left:0;background:var(--surface2);z-index:3;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);min-width:64px;width:64px;max-width:64px;text-align:center;">#</th>' +
                headers.map(function(h) {
                    return '<th style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--surface2);z-index:1;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);text-align:left;white-space:nowrap;">' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '</th>';
                }).join('') +
                '<th style="position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--surface2);z-index:2;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);min-width:110px;width:110px;max-width:110px;text-align:center;">Action</th>' +
                '</tr></thead>';

            var tbody = '<tbody>' + rows.map(function(row, idx) {
                var cols = headers.map(function(h) {
                    var v = (row && row[h] !== undefined && row[h] !== null) ? row[h] : '';
                    return '<td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);vertical-align:top;min-width:' + cellMinWidth + 'px;width:' + cellMinWidth + 'px;">' +
                        '<textarea data-map-sheet="' + adminEscapeHtml(sheetName) + '" data-map-row="' + idx + '" data-col="' + adminEscapeHtml(h) + '" rows="2" style="width:100%;min-width:' + (cellMinWidth - 16) + 'px;resize:none;overflow:hidden;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:8px;background:var(--surface);font-family:var(--sans);">' + adminEscapeHtml(v) + '</textarea>' +
                        '</td>';
                }).join('');
                return '<tr>' +
                    '<td style="position:sticky;left:0;background:var(--surface2);z-index:2;padding:8px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);font-weight:700;min-width:64px;width:64px;max-width:64px;text-align:center;">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
                    cols +
                    '<td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--stroke2);vertical-align:top;min-width:110px;width:110px;max-width:110px;">' +
                    '<button type="button" class="btn ghost adminRemoveMapRowBtn" data-sheet-name="' + adminEscapeHtml(sheetName) + '" data-row-index="' + idx + '" style="padding:6px 10px;">Remove</button>' +
                    '</td>' +
                    '</tr>';
            }).join('') + '</tbody>';

            wrap.innerHTML = title + '<div class="admin-map-scroll" style="overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:auto;max-width:100%;max-height:360px;border:1px solid var(--stroke);border-radius:10px;background:var(--surface2);-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;overscroll-behavior-x:contain;cursor:grab;">' +
                '<table style="border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:auto;width:max-content;min-width:' + tableMinWidth + 'px;">' + thead + tbody + '</table></div>';

            host.appendChild(wrap);

            var scrollWrap = wrap.querySelector('.admin-map-scroll');
            if (scrollWrap) {
                var drag = {
                    active: false,
                    startX: 0,
                    startY: 0,
                    left: 0,
                    top: 0,
                    pointerId: null,
                    moved: false
                };

                scrollWrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
                    if (e.button !== 0 && e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
                    if (e.target && e.target.closest('textarea,button,input,select,label,a')) return;
                    drag.active = true;
                    drag.pointerId = e.pointerId;
                    drag.startX = e.clientX;
                    drag.startY = e.clientY;
                    drag.left = scrollWrap.scrollLeft;
                    drag.top = scrollWrap.scrollTop;
                    drag.moved = false;
                    scrollWrap.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
                    if (typeof scrollWrap.setPointerCapture === 'function') {
                        scrollWrap.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
                    }
                });

                scrollWrap.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e) {
                    if (!drag.active) return;
                    var dx = e.clientX - drag.startX;
                    var dy = e.clientY - drag.startY;
                    if (!drag.moved && (Math.abs(dx) > 2 || Math.abs(dy) > 2)) drag.moved = true;
                    scrollWrap.scrollLeft = drag.left - dx;
                    scrollWrap.scrollTop = drag.top - dy;
                    e.preventDefault();
                });

                function endDrag(e) {
                    if (!drag.active) return;
                    drag.active = false;
                    scrollWrap.style.cursor = 'grab';
                    if (e && drag.pointerId != null && typeof scrollWrap.releasePointerCapture === 'function') {
                        try {
                            scrollWrap.releasePointerCapture(drag.pointerId);
                        } catch (_err) {}
                    }
                    drag.pointerId = null;
                }

                scrollWrap.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag);
                scrollWrap.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);
                scrollWrap.addEventListener('pointerleave', function(e) {
                    if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse') return;
                    endDrag(e);
                });
            }
        }

        function adminRenderMappingBundle(tables) {
            var host = document.getElementById('adminMappingRows');
            if (!host) return;
            host.innerHTML = '';

            var list = Array.isArray(tables) ? tables : [];
            if (!list.length) {
                host.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);">No linked mapping sheets configured.</div>';
                return;
            }

            window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet = {};
            list.forEach(function(cfg) {
                var rows = Array.isArray(cfg.rows) ? cfg.rows : [];
                window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[cfg.sheetName] = rows;
                adminRenderMappingRowsTable_(cfg, rows);
            });

            host.querySelectorAll('.adminAddMapRowBtn').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    var sheet = btn.getAttribute('data-sheet-name');
                    var rows = window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[sheet] || [];
                    rows.push({});
                    window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[sheet] = rows;
                    adminRenderMappingBundle(window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs.map(function(cfg) {
                        return Object.assign({}, cfg, {
                            rows: window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[cfg.sheetName] || []
                        });
                    }));
                });
            });


            host.querySelectorAll('.adminRemoveMapRowBtn').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    var sheet = btn.getAttribute('data-sheet-name');
                    var idx = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-row-index'));
                    var rows = window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[sheet] || [];
                    rows.splice(idx, 1);
                    if (!rows.length) rows = [{}];
                    window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[sheet] = rows;
                    adminRenderMappingBundle(window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs.map(function(cfg) {
                        return Object.assign({}, cfg, {
                            rows: window.AdminEditor.mappingRowsBySheet[cfg.sheetName] || []
                        });
                    }));
                });
            });

            adminBindAutosize(host);
        }

        function adminCollectMappingRows() {
            var host = document.getElementById('adminMappingRows');
            if (!host) return {};
            var out = {};
            host.querySelectorAll('textarea[data-map-sheet][data-map-row][data-col]').forEach(function(el) {
                var sheet = String(el.getAttribute('data-map-sheet'));
                var rowIdx = Number(el.getAttribute('data-map-row'));
                var col = el.getAttribute('data-col');
                if (!out[sheet]) out[sheet] = [];
                if (!out[sheet][rowIdx]) out[sheet][rowIdx] = {};
                out[sheet][rowIdx][col] = el.value;
            });
            return out;
        }

        function adminLoadMappingsByQuestionId() {
            var qInput = document.getElementById('adminGroupQuestionId');
            var qid = qInput ? String(qInput.value || '').trim() : '';

            if (!qid) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('Enter a Question_ID first.', true);
                return;
            }


            adminGsRun('cpos_getQuestionMappingsBundle', qid, 500).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) {
                    adminSetMappingStatus('Failed to load linked mappings.', true);
                    return;
                }
                var tables = (resp.tables || []).filter(function(t) {
                    return t.exists !== false;
                });
                if (!tables.length) {
                    adminSetMappingStatus('No linked mapping sheets found.', true);
                    return;
                }
                window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs = tables.map(function(t) {
                    return {
                        sheetName: t.sheetName,
                        linkColumn: t.linkColumn,
                        matchMode: t.matchMode,
                        headers: t.headers || []
                    };
                });
                adminRenderMappingBundle(tables);
                var total = tables.reduce(function(acc, t) {
                    return acc + Number(t.count || 0);
                }, 0);
                adminSetMappingStatus('Loaded ' + total + ' linked row(s) across ' + tables.length + ' sheet(s) for ' + qid + '.', false);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('Load mappings failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }



        function adminSaveMappings() {
            var qid = ((document.getElementById('adminGroupQuestionId') || {}).value || '').trim();
            if (!qid) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('Enter a Question_ID first.', true);
                return;
            }
            var rowsBySheet = adminCollectMappingRows();
            var tables = (window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs || []).map(function(cfg) {
                var rows = (rowsBySheet[cfg.sheetName] || []).filter(function(r) {
                    return r && Object.keys(r).length > 0;
                });
                return {
                    sheetName: cfg.sheetName,
                    rows: rows
                };
            }).filter(function(x) {
                return x.rows.length > 0;
            });

            if (!tables.length) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('No mapping rows to save.', true);
                return;
            }

            adminGsRun('cpos_saveQuestionMappingsBundle', {
                questionId: qid,
                tables: tables
            }).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) {
                    adminSetMappingStatus('Save mappings failed.', true);
                    return;
                }
                adminSetMappingStatus('Saved linked mappings. Created: ' + resp.created + ', Updated: ' + resp.updated + '.', false);
                adminRefreshCurrentTable();
            }).catch(function(err) {
                adminSetMappingStatus('Save mappings failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }

        function adminLoadConfigAndTable(forceTableName) {
            return adminGsRun('cpos_getAdvancedAdminConfig').then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load admin config');
                var select = document.getElementById('adminSheetSelect');
                if (!select) return;

                window.AdminEditor.configByTable = {};
                (resp.tables || []).forEach(function(t) {
                    window.AdminEditor.configByTable[t.name] = t;
                });

                if (!select.dataset.loaded) {
                    select.innerHTML = (resp.tables || []).filter(function(t) {
                        return !!t.exists;
                    }).map(function(t) {
                        return '<option value="' + adminEscapeHtml(t.name) + '">' + adminEscapeHtml(t.name) + '</option>';
                    }).join('');
                    select.dataset.loaded = '1';
                }

                var tableName = forceTableName || select.value;
                if (!tableName) return;
                window.AdminEditor.currentTable = tableName;
                window.AdminEditor.currentConfig = window.AdminEditor.configByTable[tableName] || null;
                adminSetInfo(window.AdminEditor.currentConfig);
                return adminRefreshCurrentTable();
            });
        }

        function adminRefreshCurrentTable() {
            var tableName = window.AdminEditor.currentTable || (document.getElementById('adminSheetSelect') || {}).value;
            if (!tableName) return Promise.resolve();

            return adminGsRun('cpos_getAdvancedAdminTableRows', tableName, 80).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load table rows');
                var headers = resp.headers || [];
                window.AdminEditor.currentHeaders = headers;
                adminRenderForm(headers, {});
                adminRenderPreview(resp.rows || [], headers);
                adminToggleMappingEditor();

                if (adminSupportsQuestionMappings() && (window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs || []).length) {
                    adminRenderMappingBundle((window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs || []).map(function(cfg) {
                        return Object.assign({}, cfg, {
                            rows: []
                        });
                    }));
                }
                window.AdminEditor.loadedRowNumber = null;
                adminSetStatus('Loaded ' + (resp.rows || []).length + ' row(s).', false);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                adminSetStatus('Load failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }

        function adminFetchById() {
            var idInput = document.getElementById('adminLookupId');
            var idVal = idInput ? String(idInput.value || '').trim() : '';
            var tableName = window.AdminEditor.currentTable;
            var cfg = window.AdminEditor.currentConfig;

            if (!tableName) {
                adminSetStatus('Select a table first.', true);
                return;
            }
            if (!cfg || !cfg.idColumn) {
                adminSetStatus('This table does not support ID lookup.', true);
                return;
            }
            if (!idVal) {
                adminSetStatus('Please enter an ID value.', true);
                return;
            }

            adminGsRun('cpos_getAdvancedAdminRowById', tableName, idVal).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok || !resp.found) {
                    adminSetStatus('Row not found for ID: ' + idVal, true);
                    return;
                }
                adminRenderForm(resp.headers || Object.keys(resp.row || {}), resp.row || {});
                window.AdminEditor.loadedRowNumber = resp.rowNumber || null;
                adminSetStatus('Loaded row #' + resp.rowNumber + ' for edit.', false);
            }).catch(function(err) {
                adminSetStatus('Fetch failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }

        function adminSaveCurrentRow() {
            var tableName = window.AdminEditor.currentTable;
            if (!tableName) {
                adminSetStatus('Select a table first.', true);
                return;
            }
            var row = adminCollectFormRow();
            adminGsRun('cpos_upsertAdvancedAdminRow', {
                sheetName: tableName,
                row: row
            }).then(function(resp) {
                if (!resp || !resp.ok) {
                    adminSetStatus('Save failed.', true);
                    return;
                }
                var msg = (resp.action === 'updated' ? 'Updated' : 'Created') + ' row #' + resp.rowNumber;
                if (resp.idColumn && resp.idValue) {
                    msg += ' • ' + resp.idColumn + ': ' + resp.idValue;
                    var lookup = document.getElementById('adminLookupId');
                    if (lookup) lookup.value = resp.idValue;
                }
                adminSetStatus(msg, false);
                adminRefreshCurrentTable();
            }).catch(function(err) {
                adminSetStatus('Save failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }

        function initAdminModule() {
            if (window.AdminEditor.initialized) return;

            var tabQuestionBtn = document.getElementById('adminTabQuestionBtn');
            var tabEditorBtn = document.getElementById('adminTabEditorBtn');
            var tabHelpBtn = document.getElementById('adminTabHelpBtn');
            var tabEditor = document.getElementById('adminTabEditor');
            var tabHelp = document.getElementById('adminTabHelp');

            var qePanel = document.getElementById('adminQuestionEditorPanel');
            var rawPanel = document.getElementById('adminRawEditorPanel');

            function setAdminTopTab(mode) {
                if (tabEditor) tabEditor.style.display = (mode === 'help') ? 'none' : 'block';
                if (tabHelp) tabHelp.style.display = (mode === 'help') ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (qePanel) qePanel.style.display = (mode === 'question') ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (rawPanel) rawPanel.style.display = (mode === 'raw') ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (tabQuestionBtn) tabQuestionBtn.className = 'btn ' + (mode === 'question' ? 'primary' : 'ghost');
                if (tabEditorBtn) tabEditorBtn.className = 'btn ' + (mode === 'raw' ? 'primary' : 'ghost');
                if (tabHelpBtn) tabHelpBtn.className = 'btn ' + (mode === 'help' ? 'primary' : 'ghost');
            }

            if (tabQuestionBtn) tabQuestionBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                setAdminTopTab('question');
            });
            if (tabEditorBtn) tabEditorBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                setAdminTopTab('raw');
            });
            if (tabHelpBtn) tabHelpBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                setAdminTopTab('help');
            });
            setAdminTopTab('question');

            var select = document.getElementById('adminSheetSelect');
            if (select) {
                select.addEventListener('change', function() {
                    window.AdminEditor.currentTable = select.value;
                    window.AdminEditor.currentConfig = window.AdminEditor.configByTable[select.value] || null;
                    adminSetInfo(window.AdminEditor.currentConfig);
                    adminRefreshCurrentTable();
                });
            }

            var fetchBtn = document.getElementById('adminFetchBtn');
            if (fetchBtn) fetchBtn.addEventListener('click', adminFetchById);
            var refreshBtn = document.getElementById('adminRefreshBtn');
            if (refreshBtn) refreshBtn.addEventListener('click', adminRefreshCurrentTable);
            var saveBtn = document.getElementById('adminSaveBtn');
            if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener('click', adminSaveCurrentRow);
            var newBtn = document.getElementById('adminNewBtn');
            if (newBtn) newBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                adminFillForm({});
                var lookup = document.getElementById('adminLookupId');
                if (lookup) lookup.value = '';
                adminSetStatus('Ready for new row. Leave ID blank for auto-generation.', false);
            });

            var loadMappingsBtn = document.getElementById('adminLoadMappingsBtn');
            if (loadMappingsBtn) loadMappingsBtn.addEventListener('click', adminLoadMappingsByQuestionId);

            var saveMappingsBtn = document.getElementById('adminSaveMappingsBtn');
            if (saveMappingsBtn) saveMappingsBtn.addEventListener('click', adminSaveMappings);

            adminGsRun('cpos_getQuestionMappingSheetsConfig').then(function(resp) {
                if (resp && resp.ok) {
                    window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs = (resp.tables || []).filter(function(t) {
                        return t.exists !== false;
                    });
                } else {
                    window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs = [];
                }
                adminToggleMappingEditor();
            }).catch(function() {
                window.AdminEditor.mappingSheetConfigs = [];
                adminToggleMappingEditor();
            });


            initAdminQuestionEditor();
            window.AdminEditor.initialized = true;
            adminLoadConfigAndTable().catch(function(err) {
                adminSetStatus('Admin init failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), true);
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            if (currentModule === 'admin') {
                initAdminModule();
            }
        });

        /**
         * Discover available domains from question bank
         */
        function discoverAvailableDomains() {
            var domainCounts = {}

            ;

            Array.from(CPOSL.q.idx.qBankById.values()).forEach(function(q) {
                    if (q.Is_Active === false) return;
                    var domain = extractDomain(q.Question_ID);
                    domainCounts[domain] = (domainCounts[domain] || 0) + 1;
                }

            );

            // Sort by count (most questions first)
            var sortedDomains = Object.keys(domainCounts).filter(function(d) {
                    return domainCounts[d] > 0;
                }

            ).sort(function(a, b) {
                    return domainCounts[b] - domainCounts[a];
                }

            );

            console.log('[Discovery] Available domains:', domainCounts);
            console.log('[Discovery] Sorted by count:', sortedDomains);

            return sortedDomains;
        }

        // UTILITIES
        function setStatus(ok, msg) {
            const dot = document.getElementById('statusDot');
            const text = document.getElementById('statusText');

            if (dot) {
                dot.classList.remove('ok', 'bad');
                dot.classList.add(ok ? 'ok' : 'bad');
            }

            if (text) text.textContent = msg || (ok ? 'Ready' : 'Error');
        }

        function goStep(n) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(el) {
                    var isActive = Number(el.dataset.step) === n;
                    el.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                }

            );

            for (var i = 0; i <= 3; i++) {
                var panel = document.getElementById('panel' + i);
                if (panel) panel.classList.toggle('active', i === n);
            }
        }

        function escapeHtml(s) {
            return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#039;');

        }

        function toNum(v) {
            if (v == null) return null;
            var n = Number(v);
            return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
        }

        function toBool(v) {
            if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v;
            var s = String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            return s === 'true' || s === '1' || s === 'yes';
        }

        function normKey(s) {
            return String(s == null ? '' : s).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
        }

        function getField(row, keys, fallback) {
            if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : '';

            for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
                var k = keys[i];

                if (row[k] !== undefined && row[k] !== null && String(row[k]).trim() !== '') {
                    return row[k];
                }
            }

            return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : '';
        }

        // CONSISTENCY RULES - Validate answer coherence
        var CONSISTENCY_RULES = [{

                id: 'RULE_SOIL_01',
                name: 'Drainage-Texture Contradiction',
                check: function(signals) {
                    var drainage = signals['DRAINAGE'] ? signals['DRAINAGE'].value : null;
                    var texture = signals['TEXTURE'] ? signals['TEXTURE'].value : null;

                    if ((drainage === 'POOR' || drainage === 'VERYPOOR') && (texture === 'SANDY' || texture === 'LIGHTSANDY')) {
                        return {
                            valid: false,
                            severity: 'HIGH',
                            message: 'Sandy soils typically drain quickly. If water stays for 2-3 days, your soil likely has some clay content.',
                            conflictingQuestions: ['Q_S015', 'Q_S008']
                        }

                        ;
                    }

                    return {
                        valid: true
                    }

                    ;
                }
            }

            ,
            {

                id: 'RULE_SOIL_02',
                name: 'Compaction-Tillage Contradiction',
                check: function(signals) {
                    var compaction = signals['COMPACTION'] ? signals['COMPACTION'].value : null;
                    var tillage = signals['TILLAGE'] ? signals['TILLAGE'].value : null;

                    if (compaction === 'HIGH' && tillage === 'ZEROTILL') {
                        return {
                            valid: false,
                            severity: 'MEDIUM',
                            message: 'Zero-till practices can initially cause surface compaction, but after 2-3 years it typically improves.',
                            conflictingQuestions: ['Q_M021', 'Q_M009']
                        }

                        ;
                    }

                    return {
                        valid: true
                    }

                    ;
                }
            }

            ,
            {

                id: 'RULE_NUMERIC_01',
                name: 'SOC Physical Bounds',
                check: function(signals) {
                    var socSignal = signals['SOC_MEASURED'];
                    var socValue = socSignal ? parseFloat(socSignal.value) : null;

                    if (socValue && socValue > 10) {
                        return {
                            valid: false,
                            severity: 'HIGH',
                            message: 'Soil organic carbon above 10% is rare in mineral soils. Please verify the value.',
                            conflictingQuestions: ['Q_S003']
                        }

                        ;
                    }

                    return {
                        valid: true
                    }

                    ;
                }
            }

            ,
            {

                id: 'RULE_CROP_01',
                name: 'Rice-Rainfed Contradiction',
                check: function(signals) {
                    var hasCrop = function(cropName) {
                        return Object.keys(signals).some(function(k) {
                                return k.indexOf('CROP') >= 0 && signals[k].value && String(signals[k].value).toUpperCase().indexOf(cropName) >= 0;
                            }

                        );
                    }

                    ;

                    var irrigation = signals['IRRIGATION'] ? signals['IRRIGATION'].value : null;

                    if (hasCrop('RICE') && irrigation === 'NONE') {
                        return {
                            valid: false,
                            severity: 'MEDIUM',
                            message: 'Rice usually needs consistent water. Are you growing upland/aerobic rice, or is your area naturally flooded during monsoon?',
                            conflictingQuestions: ['Q_A1', 'Q_M015']
                        }

                        ;
                    }

                    return {
                        valid: true
                    }

                    ;
                }
            }

            ,
            {

                id: 'RULE_MGT_01',
                name: 'High Nitrogen - Low Yield Contradiction',
                check: function(signals) {
                    var nRate = toNum(signals['NITROGEN_RATE'] ? signals['NITROGEN_RATE'].value : null);
                    var yieldVal = toNum(signals['CROP_YIELD'] ? signals['CROP_YIELD'].value : null);
                    var crop = signals['CROP'] ? signals['CROP'].value : null;

                    if (nRate && nRate > 200 && yieldVal && yieldVal < 3 && (crop === 'RICE' || crop === 'WHEAT' || crop === 'MAIZE')) {
                        return {
                            valid: false,
                            severity: 'HIGH',
                            message: 'High nitrogen (' + nRate + ' kg/ha) with low yield (' + yieldVal + ' t/ha) suggests inefficiency. Please verify.',
                            conflictingQuestions: ['Q_M019', 'Q_A003']
                        }

                        ;
                    }

                    return {
                        valid: true
                    }

                    ;
                }
            }

        ];

        function checkAnswerConsistency() {
            var violations = [];

            CONSISTENCY_RULES.forEach(function(rule) {
                    try {
                        var result = rule.check(CPOSL.qState.signals);

                        if (!result.valid) {
                            violations.push({
                                    ruleId: rule.id,
                                    ruleName: rule.name,
                                    severity: result.severity,
                                    message: result.message,
                                    conflictingQuestions: result.conflictingQuestions || []
                                }

                            );
                        }
                    } catch (err) {
                        console.warn('[Consistency] Rule ' + rule.id + ' failed:', err);
                    }
                }

            );

            return violations;
        }

        function showConsistencyWarning(violation) {
            var lang = CPOSL.language;
            // Store violation first so reviewConflict() can read it from state (ruleId not embedded in onclick)
            CPOSL.qState.pendingWarning = violation;
            var warningHtml = '<div class="warning-box">' + '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start">' + '<span style="font-size:24px">⚠️</span>' + '<div style="flex:1">' + '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px;color:var(--warn)">' + (lang === 'HI' ? 'त्वरित जाँच' : 'Quick Check') + '</div>' + '<div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;margin-bottom:10px">' + escapeHtml(violation.message) + '</div>' + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">' + '<button class="btn ghost" id="btnReviewConflict">' + (lang === 'HI' ? 'पिछले उत्तर देखें' : 'Review Previous Answers') + '</button>' + '<button class="btn primary" onclick="continueAnyway()">' + (lang === 'HI' ? 'फिर भी जारी रखें' : 'Continue Anyway') + '</button>' + '</div></div></div></div>';

            document.getElementById('qNavStatus').innerHTML = warningHtml;
            // Attach reviewer click handler after DOM insertion (avoids ruleId injection into onclick attribute)
            var btnReview = document.getElementById('btnReviewConflict');
            if (btnReview) {
                btnReview.addEventListener('click', function() { reviewConflict(violation.ruleId); });
            }
        }

        function reviewConflict(ruleId) {
            var violation = CPOSL.qState.pendingWarning;

            if (violation && violation.conflictingQuestions && violation.conflictingQuestions.length > 0) {
                alert('Conflicting answers from questions: ' + violation.conflictingQuestions.join(', '));
            }

            document.getElementById('qNavStatus').textContent = 'Review your answers, then click Next.';
            CPOSL.qState.pendingWarning = null;
        }

        function continueAnyway() {
            var violation = CPOSL.qState.pendingWarning;

            if (violation && violation.conflictingQuestions) {
                violation.conflictingQuestions.forEach(function(qid) {
                        var ans = CPOSL.qState.answered[qid];

                        if (ans && ans.derivedSignals) {
                            Object.keys(ans.derivedSignals).forEach(function(sigKey) {
                                    if (CPOSL.qState.signals[sigKey]) {
                                        CPOSL.qState.signals[sigKey].confidence *= 0.8;
                                        CPOSL.qState.signals[sigKey].hasConflict = true;
                                    }
                                }

                            );
                        }
                    }

                );
            }

            document.getElementById('qNavStatus').textContent = '';
            CPOSL.qState.pendingWarning = null;
            proceedToNextQuestion();
        }
