                        }
                    });

                    div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') {
                            radio.checked = true;
                            radio.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                        }
                    });
                });

                answerBox.appendChild(grid);

            } else if (qType === 'number' || qType === 'numeric_direct') {
                var placeholder = lang === 'HI' ? 'संख्या दर्ज करें' : 'Enter number';
                if (q.unit) {
                    placeholder += ' (' + q.unit + ')';
                }

                var input = document.createElement('input');
                input.type = 'number';
                input.className = 'adv-numeric-input';
                input.placeholder = placeholder;
                input.step = '0.1';
                input.min = '0';

                input.addEventListener('input', function() {
                    document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = !input.value;
                });

                answerBox.appendChild(input);
            } else if (qType === 'yes_no_explain') {
                // Render Yes/No radio options + optional explanation textarea
                var grid = document.createElement('div');
                grid.className = 'adv-option-grid';

                var yesNoOpts = (q.options && q.options.length > 0) ? q.options : [
                    { code: 'YES', label_EN: 'Yes', label_HI: 'हाँ', score: 100, confidence: 0.85, icon: '✅' },
                    { code: 'NO',  label_EN: 'No',  label_HI: 'नहीं', score: 0,   confidence: 0.85, icon: '❌' }
                ];

                yesNoOpts.forEach(function(opt) {
                    var label = lang === 'HI' && opt.label_HI ? opt.label_HI : opt.label_EN;
                    var icon = opt.icon || '●';
                    var div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'adv-option-item';
                    div.innerHTML = '<div class="adv-option-icon">' + escapeHtml(icon) + '</div>' +
                        '<input type="radio" name="adv_q_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '" value="' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" ' +
                        'id="adv_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-radio" ' +
                        'data-score="' + (opt.score || 0) + '" data-confidence="' + (opt.confidence || 0.85) + '">' +
                        '<label for="adv_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-label">' + escapeHtml(label) + '</label>';

                    grid.appendChild(div);

                    var radio = div.querySelector('input');
                    radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
                        grid.querySelectorAll('.adv-option-item').forEach(function(item) { item.classList.remove('selected'); });
                        if (radio.checked) {
                            div.classList.add('selected');
                            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                        }
                    });
                    div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') { radio.checked = true; radio.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
                    });
                });

                answerBox.appendChild(grid);

                var explainLabel = document.createElement('div');
                explainLabel.style.cssText = 'margin-top:14px;font-size:13px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);';
                explainLabel.textContent = lang === 'HI' ? 'अतिरिक्त विवरण (वैकल्पिक)' : 'Explanation (optional)';
                answerBox.appendChild(explainLabel);

                var explainArea = document.createElement('textarea');
                explainArea.id = 'adv_explain_' + q.id;
                explainArea.className = 'adv-numeric-input';
                explainArea.placeholder = lang === 'HI' ? 'यहाँ लिखें...' : 'Add details here...';
                explainArea.rows = 3;
                explainArea.style.cssText = 'width:100%;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);color:#fff;font-size:14px;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box;margin-top:6px;';
                answerBox.appendChild(explainArea);
            } else if (qType === 'text_input') {
                var textArea = document.createElement('textarea');
                textArea.className = 'adv-numeric-input';
                textArea.placeholder = lang === 'HI' ? 'यहाँ लिखें...' : 'Enter your answer...';
                textArea.rows = 4;
                textArea.style.cssText = 'width:100%;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);color:#fff;font-size:14px;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box;';
                textArea.addEventListener('input', function() {
                    document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = !textArea.value.trim();
                });
                answerBox.appendChild(textArea);
            } else {
                // Unrecognised type — fall back to yes/no rather than a dead-end error
                console.warn('[Advanced] Unknown qType "' + qType + '" for', q.id, '— rendering as yes_no_explain');
                var grid2 = document.createElement('div');
                grid2.className = 'adv-option-grid';
                var fallbackOpts = (q.options && q.options.length > 0) ? q.options : [
                    { code: 'YES', label_EN: 'Yes', label_HI: 'हाँ', score: 100, confidence: 0.85, icon: '✅' },
                    { code: 'NO',  label_EN: 'No',  label_HI: 'नहीं', score: 0,   confidence: 0.85, icon: '❌' }
                ];
                fallbackOpts.forEach(function(opt) {
                    var label = lang === 'HI' && opt.label_HI ? opt.label_HI : opt.label_EN;
                    var div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'adv-option-item';
                    div.innerHTML = '<div class="adv-option-icon">' + escapeHtml(opt.icon || '●') + '</div>' +
                        '<input type="radio" name="adv_q_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '" value="' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" ' +
                        'id="adv_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-radio" ' +
                        'data-score="' + (opt.score || 0) + '" data-confidence="' + (opt.confidence || 0.85) + '">' +
                        '<label for="adv_' + escapeHtml(q.id) + '_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-label">' + escapeHtml(label) + '</label>';
                    grid2.appendChild(div);
                    var r2 = div.querySelector('input');
                    r2.addEventListener('change', function() {
                        grid2.querySelectorAll('.adv-option-item').forEach(function(i) { i.classList.remove('selected'); });
                        if (r2.checked) { div.classList.add('selected'); document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false; }
                    });
                    div.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') { r2.checked = true; r2.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); } });
                });
                answerBox.appendChild(grid2);
            }

            document.getElementById('btnAdvBack').style.display = idx > 0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').innerHTML = '<span>Next</span><span>→</span>';
            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').style.display = 'inline-flex';
            document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip').style.display = 'inline-flex';

            console.log('[Advanced] Rendered question', idx + 1, ':', q.id);
        }

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ADVANCED QUESTIONNAIRE — SEQUENCING ENGINE
        //
        // HOW QUESTION SEQUENCE IS DETERMINED
        // ====================================
        // Questions are loaded from CPOS_Advanced_Questions (or CPOS_Advanced_Q_Bank
        // as fallback). They are first filtered by pathway (e.g. 'AWD'), then sorted
        // by (Dimension_ID asc, Sequence asc). This gives the BASE LINEAR ORDER.
        //
        // After the farmer submits each answer, the sequencing pipeline runs in this
        // strict order:
        //
        //   STEP 1 — DERIVATION (CPOS_Advanced_Derivation_Rules)
        //     Checks if the answer triggers any signal derivations.
        //     A "signal" is an in-memory key=value pair stored in ADVANCED.state.signals.
        //     Signals carry meaning across questions and are consumed by routing rules.
        //
        //   STEP 2 — DISQUALIFICATION (CPOS_Advanced_Inference_Rules)
        //     Checks if the answer (by questionId + answerCode) matches an
        //     Action=DISQUALIFY inference rule. If matched, the assessment ends
        //     immediately with a disqualification screen.
        //
        //   STEP 3 — ROUTING (CPOS_Advanced_Routing_Rules)
        //     Checks if any routing rule matches the current question + the live
        //     signals state. A match can:
        //       • SKIP_TO / ASK  → jump the cursor to a specific named question
        //       • END            → jump straight to the results screen
        //     Routing always takes priority over the checkpoint (step 4).
        //
        //   STEP 4 — CHECKPOINT (only when no routing rule fired)
        //     If the next sequential question belongs to a different Dimension_ID,
        //     a per-dimension summary card is shown before continuing.
        //
        //   STEP 5 — LINEAR PROGRESSION (only when steps 3 & 4 did not redirect)
        //     currentQuestionIndex++  →  advRenderCurrentQuestion()
        //
        //
        // CONCRETE EXAMPLE (AWD pathway, 3-question mini-flow)
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        //
        //  Base linear order after sort:
        //    [0] ADV_D2_001  Dimension=D2  Sequence=1  type=categorical_direct
        //    [1] ADV_D2_002  Dimension=D2  Sequence=2  type=yes_no_explain
        //    [2] ADV_D3_001  Dimension=D3  Sequence=1  type=categorical_direct
        //
        //  Sheets:
        //    CPOS_Advanced_Derivation_Rules:
        //      When_Question_ID=ADV_D2_001 | When_Value=RAINFED | Then_Signal_Key=IRRIGATION_TYPE | Then_Signal_Value=RAINFED
        //
        //    CPOS_Advanced_Inference_Rules:
        //      Action=DISQUALIFY | Trigger_Question_ID=ADV_D2_001 | Trigger_Answer_Code=RAINFED | Reason=RAINFED
        //
        //    CPOS_Advanced_Routing_Rules:
        //      From_Question_ID=ADV_D2_001 | Condition_Signal_Key=IRRIGATION_TYPE | Condition_Operator== | Condition_Value=PUMPED | Route_Action=SKIP_TO | Next_Question_ID=ADV_D3_001
        //
        //  Scenario A — farmer answers ADV_D2_001 = "RAINFED":
        //    Step 1: IRRIGATION_TYPE = "RAINFED" signal derived
        //    Step 2: Inference rule matches → advShowDisqualification("RAINFED")  ← STOPS HERE
        //
        //  Scenario B — farmer answers ADV_D2_001 = "PUMPED":
        //    Step 1: IRRIGATION_TYPE = "PUMPED" signal derived
        //    Step 2: No disqualification match
        //    Step 3: Routing rule matches (IRRIGATION_TYPE = PUMPED) → jump to ADV_D3_001 (idx=2)
        //            ADV_D2_002 is skipped entirely
        //    Step 4: Not reached (routing fired)
        //
        //  Scenario C — farmer answers ADV_D2_001 = "CANAL" (no routing rule for it):
        //    Step 1: No matching derivation rule → no signal emitted
        //    Step 2: No disqualification match
        //    Step 3: No routing rule matches → routing returns null
        //    Step 4: questions[1]=ADV_D2_002 is same dimension D2 → no checkpoint
        //    Step 5: currentQuestionIndex++ → show ADV_D2_002
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Check whether the current question's dimension has an entry gate that is not yet satisfied.
         * If the gate signal is not satisfied, the entire dimension should be skipped.
         * Returns true if the dimension should be skipped (gate NOT met), false if it should be entered.
         *
         * Entry gate columns from CPOS_Advanced_Dimension_Order:
         *   Entry_Gate_Signal    — signal key to check (empty / "NONE" / "ANY" = always enter)
         *   Entry_Gate_Operator  — comparison: EQUALS, !=, IN, IS_SET, IS_NOT_SET
         *   Entry_Gate_Value     — expected value (comma or | separated for IN)
         *
         * Also honours Applies_To_Pathways (| or comma separated): if set and does not
         * include the current pathway, the entire dimension is skipped regardless of signals.
         * Is_Active = FALSE rows in the sheet are treated as permanently skipped.
         */
        function advCheckDimensionEntryGate(dimension, pathway) {
    var flow = ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.dimFlowById;
    if (!flow) return false;
    var cfg = flow[dimension];
    if (!cfg) return false;

    // ----------------------------
    // Active flag
    // ----------------------------
    var isActive = String(cfg.Is_Active || cfg.IsActive || 'TRUE').toUpperCase();
    if (isActive === 'FALSE') {
        console.log('[Advanced] Skipping dimension', dimension, '— Is_Active = FALSE');
        return true; // true => skip dimension
    }

    // ----------------------------
    // ALL4-aware pathway applicability
    // ----------------------------
    var appliesToRaw = String(cfg.Applies_To_Pathways || cfg.ApplyToPathways || 'ALL').trim().toUpperCase();

    function toTokenSet(v) {
        var out = [];
        function pushToken(t) {
            t = String(t || '').trim().toUpperCase();
            if (!t) return;
            if (out.indexOf(t) < 0) out.push(t);
        }
        function addTokens(x) {
            if (x == null) return;
            if (Object.prototype.toString.call(x) === '[object Array]') {
                for (var i = 0; i < x.length; i++) addTokens(x[i]);
                return;
            }
            String(x).split(/[|,]/).forEach(function(part) { pushToken(part); });
        }
        addTokens(v);

        // Expand ALL / ALL4 into explicit pathway membership
        if (out.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || out.indexOf('ALL4') >= 0) {
            pushToken('ALL4');
            pushToken('AWD');
            pushToken('REGEN');
            pushToken('BIOCHAR');
            pushToken('ERW');
        }
        return out;
    }

    // Prefer state.pathwaySet if present; fallback chain remains backward-compatible
    var activePathways = toTokenSet(
        (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet) ||
        (ADVANCED.state && (ADVANCED.state.pathwayMode || ADVANCED.state.pathway)) ||
        pathway ||
        'ALL4'
    );

    var applicablePathways = toTokenSet(appliesToRaw || 'ALL');

    // If dimension is not applicable to the active pathway set => skip dimension
    // (Fail-open if we cannot resolve active pathways.)
    if (activePathways.length && applicablePathways.length) {
        // If appliesToRaw is ALL/ALL4, it's automatically applicable
        if (applicablePathways.indexOf('ALL') < 0 && applicablePathways.indexOf('ALL4') < 0) {
            var overlap = false;
            for (var ap = 0; ap < applicablePathways.length; ap++) {
                if (activePathways.indexOf(applicablePathways[ap]) >= 0) { overlap = true; break; }
            }
            if (!overlap) {
                console.log('[Advanced][GateCheck] Skipping by pathway applicability', {
                    dimension: dimension,
                    appliesToRaw: appliesToRaw,
                    activePathwayArg: pathway,
                    activePathways: activePathways,
                    applicablePathways: applicablePathways,
                    reason: 'PATHWAY_NOT_APPLICABLE'
                });
                console.log('[Advanced] Skipping dimension', dimension, '— not applicable to pathway set', activePathways.join('|'));
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    // ----------------------------
    // Entry gate config
    // ----------------------------
    var entrySignal = String(cfg.Entry_Gate_Signal || cfg.EntryGateSignal || '').trim();
    if (!entrySignal || entrySignal === 'NONE' || entrySignal === 'ANY') {
        console.log('[Advanced][GateCheck]', {
            dimension: dimension,
            appliesTo: (cfg.Applies_To_Pathways || cfg.ApplyToPathways || 'ALL'),
            activePathwayArg: pathway,
            activePathwaySet: (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet) || null,
            entrySignal: entrySignal || '(none)',
            operator: '(none)',
            entryValue: '',
            signalPresent: null,
            signalValue: '',
            conditionMet: true,
            willSkipDimension: false,
            reason: 'NO_ENTRY_GATE'
        });
        return false; // allow dimension
    }

    var op = String(cfg.Entry_Gate_Operator || cfg.EntryGateOperator || 'EQUALS').toUpperCase().trim();
    var entryVal = String(cfg.Entry_Gate_Value || cfg.EntryGateValue || '').trim();

    var sigStore = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.signals) || {};
    var sig = sigStore[entrySignal];

    function readSignalValue(signalObj) {
        if (signalObj == null) return '';
        if (typeof signalObj === 'object' && signalObj !== null && ('value' in signalObj)) {
            return String(signalObj.value == null ? '' : signalObj.value).trim();
        }
        return String(signalObj).trim();
    }

    function toUpper(v) { return String(v || '').trim().toUpperCase(); }

    function boolFromValue(v) {
        var u = toUpper(v);
        if (!u) return null;
        if (['YES', 'Y', 'TRUE', '1'].indexOf(u) >= 0) return true;
        if (['NO', 'N', 'FALSE', '0'].indexOf(u) >= 0) return false;
        return null;
    }

    function compareSignal(rawSig, rawExpected, operator) {
        var s = String(rawSig || '').trim();
        var e = String(rawExpected || '').trim();

        if (operator === 'EQUALS' || operator === '=') {
            if (s === e) return true;
            if (toUpper(s) === toUpper(e) && (s || e)) return true;

            var expectedBool = boolFromValue(e);
            if (expectedBool !== null) {
                var signalBool = boolFromValue(s);
                if (signalBool === null) signalBool = !!s;
                return signalBool === expectedBool;
            }
            return false;
        }

        if (operator === '!=' || operator === 'NOT_EQUALS') {
            return !compareSignal(rawSig, rawExpected, 'EQUALS');
        }

        return s === e;
    }

    // ----------------------------
    // CRITICAL CHANGE: defer by skipping when signal not present
    // ----------------------------
    if (!sig && op !== 'IS_NOT_SET' && op !== 'IS_SET') {
        console.log('[Advanced][GateCheck]', {
            dimension: dimension,
            appliesTo: (cfg.Applies_To_Pathways || cfg.ApplyToPathways || 'ALL'),
            activePathwayArg: pathway,
            activePathwaySet: (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet) || null,
            entrySignal: entrySignal,
            operator: op,
            entryValue: entryVal,
            signalPresent: false,
            signalValue: '',
            conditionMet: null,
            willSkipDimension: true,
            reason: 'DEFER_UNTIL_SIGNAL_SET'
        });
        console.log('[Advanced] Deferring dimension', dimension, '— gate signal', entrySignal, 'not yet set');
        return true; // ✅ skip for now, revisit later when signal exists
    }

    var sigVal = readSignalValue(sig);

    // ----------------------------
    // Evaluate gate
    // ----------------------------
    var conditionMet = false;

    if (op === 'EQUALS' || op === '=' || op === '!=' || op === 'NOT_EQUALS') {
        conditionMet = compareSignal(sigVal, entryVal, op);
    } else if (op === 'IN') {
        if (entryVal) {
            var inVals = entryVal.split(/[|,]/).map(function(v) { return v.trim().toUpperCase(); });
            conditionMet = inVals.indexOf(toUpper(sigVal)) >= 0;
        } else {
            conditionMet = false;
        }
    } else if (op === 'IS_SET') {
        conditionMet = !!sig;
    } else if (op === 'IS_NOT_SET') {
        conditionMet = !sig;
    } else {
        conditionMet = compareSignal(sigVal, entryVal, 'EQUALS');
    }

    if (!conditionMet) {
        console.log('[Advanced] Skipping dimension', dimension, '— entry gate not met:',
            entrySignal, op, entryVal, '(current:', sigVal || '(not set)', ')');
    }

    console.log('[Advanced][GateCheck]', {
        dimension: dimension,
        appliesTo: (cfg.Applies_To_Pathways || cfg.ApplyToPathways || 'ALL'),
        activePathwayArg: pathway,
        activePathwaySet: (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet) || null,
        entrySignal: entrySignal,
        operator: op,
        entryValue: entryVal,
        signalPresent: !!sig,
        signalValue: sigVal,
        conditionMet: conditionMet,
        willSkipDimension: !conditionMet,
        reason: conditionMet ? 'GATE_MET' : 'GATE_NOT_MET'
    });

    return !conditionMet; // true => skip, false => allow
}

        /**
         * Apply derivation rules: derive signals from the current answer using CPOS_Advanced_Derivation_Rules
         */
        function advApplyDerivationRules(questionId, answerCode) {
            var rules = ADVANCED.state.derivationRules || [];
            // Sort by Priority ascending (lower number = higher priority); same convention as routing rules
            var sorted = rules.slice().sort(function(a, b) {
                return Number(a.Priority || a.priority || 999) - Number(b.Priority || b.priority || 999);
            });
            var answerNum = parseFloat(answerCode); // for numeric comparisons; NaN for categorical answers
            sorted.forEach(function(rule) {
                var isActive = String(rule.Is_Active || rule.IsActive || 'TRUE').toUpperCase();
                if (isActive === 'FALSE') return;
                var whenQ = rule.When_Question_ID || rule.WhenQuestionID || '';
                if (whenQ !== questionId) return;
                var op = String(rule.When_Operator || '=').toUpperCase().trim();
                var whenVal = String(rule.When_Value || '');
                var numMin = parseFloat(rule.When_Numeric_Min);
                var numMax = parseFloat(rule.When_Numeric_Max);
                var matches = false;
                if (op === '=' || op === 'EQUALS') {
                    matches = answerCode === whenVal;
                } else if (op === '!=' || op === 'NOT_EQUALS') {
                    matches = answerCode !== whenVal;
                } else if (op === 'IN') {
                    matches = whenVal.split(/[|,]/).map(function(v) { return v.trim(); }).indexOf(answerCode) >= 0;
                } else if (op === 'BETWEEN') {
                    // Uses When_Numeric_Min and When_Numeric_Max columns
                    matches = !isNaN(answerNum) && !isNaN(numMin) && !isNaN(numMax) &&
                              answerNum >= numMin && answerNum <= numMax;
                } else if (op === 'GTE' || op === '>=') {
                    matches = !isNaN(answerNum) && answerNum >= parseFloat(whenVal);
                } else if (op === 'LTE' || op === '<=') {
                    matches = !isNaN(answerNum) && answerNum <= parseFloat(whenVal);
                } else if (op === 'GT' || op === '>') {
                    matches = !isNaN(answerNum) && answerNum > parseFloat(whenVal);
                } else if (op === 'LT' || op === '<') {
                    matches = !isNaN(answerNum) && answerNum < parseFloat(whenVal);
                } else {
                    matches = answerCode === whenVal;
                }
                if (!matches) return;
                var sigKey = rule.Then_Signal_Key || rule.ThenSignalKey || '';
                var sigVal = rule.Then_Signal_Value || rule.ThenSignalValue || '';
                var sigScore = Number(rule.Then_Signal_Score || rule.ThenSignalScore || 0);
                var confidence = Number(rule.Confidence_0_1 || rule.Confidence || 1.0);
                if (sigKey) {
                    ADVANCED.state.signals[sigKey] = { value: sigVal, score: sigScore, confidence: confidence };
                    console.log('[Advanced] Signal derived:', sigKey, '=', sigVal,
                        '(score:', sigScore, 'conf:', confidence, ')');
                }
            });
        }


  function advMarkSkipped(qid, reason) {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  st.answers = st.answers || {};
  st.questionIndexById = st.questionIndexById || {};

  qid = String(qid || '').trim();
  if (!qid) return;

  // Don't overwrite a real user answer
  if (qid in st.answers) {
    var existing = st.answers[qid];
    // If already skipped, keep first reason; otherwise don't overwrite user answer
    if (existing && existing.skipped) return;
    return;
  }

  var dim = null;

  try {
    var idxMap = st.questionIndexById;
    if (qid in idxMap) {
      var q = (st.questions || [])[idxMap[qid]];
      dim = q ? (q.dimension || q.Dimension_ID || q.Dimension || q.DimensionId || null) : null;
      if (dim) dim = String(dim).trim() || null;
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  st.answers[qid] = {
    questionId: qid,
    dimension: dim,
    answerCode: '__SKIPPED__',
    skipped: true,
    reason: reason || 'rule_skip',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

        /**
 * Apply routing rules: return the next question ID to jump to, or null for linear progression.
 * Uses CPOS_Advanced_Routing_Rules.
 */
function advApplyRoutingRules(questionId) {
  var rules = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.routingRules) || [];
  var sigStore = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.signals) || {};
  var ansStore = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.answers) || {};

  function norm(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toUpperCase(); }

  function readSigVal(sigKey) {
    var sig = sigStore[sigKey];
    if (sig == null) return '';
    if (typeof sig === 'object' && sig !== null && ('value' in sig)) {
      return String(sig.value == null ? '' : sig.value).trim();
    }
    return String(sig).trim();
  }

  function safeMarkSkipped(qid, reason) {
    qid = String(qid || '').trim();
    if (!qid) return;
    if (qid in ansStore) return;

    if (typeof advMarkSkipped === 'function') {
      advMarkSkipped(qid, reason || 'routing_skip_list');
      return;
    }

    // fallback: minimal skip object
    ansStore[qid] = { questionId: qid, answerCode: '__SKIPPED__', skipped: true, reason: reason || 'routing_skip_list' };
  }

  // Filter to active rules for this question, then sort by Priority (lower = higher priority)
  var matching = rules.filter(function(rule) {
    var isActive = norm(rule.Is_Active || rule.IsActive || 'TRUE');
    if (isActive === 'FALSE') return false;
    var fromQ = String(rule.From_Question_ID || rule.Current_Question_ID || rule.FromQuestionID || '').trim();
    return fromQ === String(questionId || '').trim();
  });

  matching.sort(function(a, b) {
    return Number(a.Priority || a.priority || 999) - Number(b.Priority || b.priority || 999);
  });

  for (var i = 0; i < matching.length; i++) {
    var rule = matching[i];

    var sigKey = String(rule.Condition_Signal_Key || rule.ConditionSignalKey || '').trim();
    var op = norm(rule.Condition_Operator || rule.ConditionOperator || '');
    var condValRaw = String(rule.Condition_Value || rule.ConditionValue || '').trim();
    var condVal = norm(condValRaw);

    var matches = false;

    if (!sigKey || norm(sigKey) === 'ANY') {
      matches = true;
    } else {
      var sigPresent = (sigKey in sigStore);
      var sigVal = norm(readSigVal(sigKey));

      // If the signal has never been set, only IS_SET/IS_NOT_SET can evaluate meaningfully.
      if (!sigPresent && op !== 'IS_SET' && op !== 'IS_NOT_SET') {
        matches = false;
      } else {
        if (op === 'IS_SET') matches = sigPresent && sigVal !== '';
        else if (op === 'IS_NOT_SET') matches = !sigPresent || sigVal === '';
        else if (op === '=' || op === 'EQUALS') matches = sigVal === condVal;
        else if (op === '!=' || op === 'NOT_EQUALS') matches = sigVal !== condVal;
        else if (op === 'IN') {
          // allow "YES|MAYBE" or "(YES,MAYBE)" etc
          var vals = condValRaw.replace(/[()]/g, '')
            .split(/[|,]/)
            .map(function(v){ return norm(v); })
            .filter(Boolean);
          matches = vals.indexOf(sigVal) >= 0;
        }
        else if (op === 'CONTAINS') matches = sigVal.indexOf(condVal) >= 0;
        else matches = sigVal === condVal;
      }
    }

    if (!matches) continue;

    var action = norm(rule.Route_Action || rule.RouteAction || 'NEXT');
    // Normalise: ASK_NEXT → ASK, SKIP → SKIP_TO
    if (action === 'ASK_NEXT') action = 'ASK';
    if (action === 'SKIP') action = 'SKIP_TO';

    // To_Question_ID is the actual sheet column; fall back to legacy names
    var nextQId = String(rule.To_Question_ID || rule.ToQuestionID || rule.Next_Question_ID || rule.NextQuestionID || '').trim();

    // Only mark Skip_Questions as __SKIPPED__ for SKIP_TO actions, not ASK.
    var skipList = String(rule.Skip_Questions || rule.SkipQuestions || '').trim();
    if (skipList && action === 'SKIP_TO') {
      skipList.split(',').forEach(function(qid) {
        safeMarkSkipped(qid, 'routing_skip_list');
      });
    }

    if (action === 'END') return { action: 'END', nextId: null };
    if ((action === 'ASK' || action === 'SKIP_TO') && nextQId) return { action: action, nextId: nextQId };
  }

  return null;
}

function advCheckInferenceDisqualifiers(answer) {
  var rules = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.inferenceRules) || [];
  if (!rules.length) return null;

  var signals = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.signals) || {};
  rules.sort(function(a, b) { return (Number(a.Priority) || 99) - (Number(b.Priority) || 99); });

  function up(x){ return String(x || '').trim().toUpperCase(); }

  for (var i = 0; i < rules.length; i++) {
    var rule = rules[i];
    if (String(rule.Is_Active || 'TRUE').trim().toUpperCase() === 'FALSE') continue;

    var trigSig = String(rule.Trigger_Signal || '').trim();
    if (!trigSig) continue;

    if (!(trigSig in signals)) continue; // signal not yet derived — skip

    var rawSig = signals[trigSig];
    var sigVal = up((rawSig && rawSig.value != null) ? rawSig.value : rawSig);

    var trigOp  = up(rule.Trigger_Operator || 'EQUALS');
    var trigVal = up(rule.Trigger_Value || '');

    var matched = false;
    if (trigOp === 'EQUALS' || trigOp === '=') {
      matched = (sigVal === trigVal);
    } else if (trigOp === '!=' || trigOp === 'NOT_EQUALS') {
      matched = (sigVal !== trigVal);
    } else if (trigOp === 'IN') {
      var vals = trigVal.replace(/[()]/g, '').split(/[|,]/).map(function(v){ return up(v); }).filter(Boolean);
      matched = (vals.indexOf(sigVal) >= 0);
    } else if (trigOp === 'IS_SET') {
      matched = (sigVal !== '');
    } else if (trigOp === 'IS_NOT_SET') {
      matched = (sigVal === '');
    }

    if (!matched) continue;

    // -----------------------
    // (A) Mark Skip_Questions as skipped (WITH dimension)
    // -----------------------
    var skipQ = String(rule.Skip_Questions || '').trim();
    if (skipQ) {
      skipQ.split(',').forEach(function(qId) {
        qId = String(qId || '').trim();
        if (!qId) return;

        // do not overwrite a real answered question
        var existing = ADVANCED.state.answers && ADVANCED.state.answers[qId];
        if (existing && !existing.skipped) return;

        if (typeof advMarkSkipped === 'function') {
          advMarkSkipped(qId, 'inference:' + String(rule.Inference_ID || rule.InferenceId || ''));
        } else {
          // fallback (kept only if advMarkSkipped is missing)
          ADVANCED.state.answers[qId] = { questionId: qId, answerCode: '__SKIPPED__', skipped: true, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
        }
      });
    }

    // -----------------------
    // (B) Emit inferred signal
    // -----------------------
    var inferSig = String(rule.Infer_Signal || '').trim();
    var inferVal = String(rule.Infer_Value || '').trim();

    if (inferSig && inferVal) {
      var conf = parseFloat(rule.Confidence_0_1 || '0.85') || 0.85;

      ADVANCED.state.signals[inferSig] = {
        value: inferVal,
        score: conf,
        confidence: conf,
        source: 'inference_rule',
        ruleId: String(rule.Inference_ID || rule.InferenceId || '')
      };

      console.log('[Advanced] Inference rule fired:', (rule.Inference_ID || rule.InferenceId), '→', inferSig, '=', inferVal);
    }
  }

  return null;
}

 function advTokenizePathways(value) {
  var out = [];

  function normTok(t) {
    t = String(t || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!t) return '';

    // Common aliases
    if (t === 'REGENERATIVE' || t === 'REGENERATION') t = 'REGEN';
    if (t === 'BIO_CHAR' || t === 'BIO-CHAR') t = 'BIOCHAR';
    if (t === 'E.R.W' || t === 'ENHANCED_ROCK_WEATHERING') t = 'ERW';
    if (t === 'A.W.D') t = 'AWD';

    return t;
  }

  function pushTok(t) {
    t = normTok(t);
    if (!t) return;
    if (out.indexOf(t) < 0) out.push(t);
  }

  function add(v) {
    if (v == null) return;
    if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === '[object Array]') {
      for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) add(v[i]);
      return;
    }
    String(v).split(/[|,]/).forEach(function(p) { pushTok(p); });
  }

  add(value);

  // Expand ALL / ALL4
  if (out.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || out.indexOf('ALL4') >= 0) {
    pushTok('ALL4');
    pushTok('AWD');
    pushTok('REGEN');
    pushTok('BIOCHAR');
    pushTok('ERW');
  }

  return out;
}

function advQuestionApplicableToCurrentPathwaySet(q) {
  if (!q) return true;

  // Raw pathways from question
  var qPathsRaw = q.pathway || q.Pathway || q.Pathways || q.Applies_To_Pathways || q.Required_For_Pathway || 'ALL';

  // Tokenize using your existing helper if present
  var qSet = (typeof advTokenizePathways === 'function') ? advTokenizePathways(qPathsRaw) : [];

  // If tokenizer returns empty, treat as ALL (fail-open)
  if (!qSet || !qSet.length) return true;

  // If question is ALL/ALL4, always applicable
  if (qSet.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || qSet.indexOf('ALL4') >= 0) return true;

  // Active pathways set from state
  var activeRaw = (ADVANCED.state && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet && ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet.length)
    ? ADVANCED.state.pathwaySet
    : (ADVANCED.state ? (ADVANCED.state.pathwayMode || ADVANCED.state.pathway) : null);

  var activeSet = (typeof advTokenizePathways === 'function') ? advTokenizePathways(activeRaw) : [];

  // If cannot resolve active set, fail-open
  if (!activeSet || !activeSet.length) return true;

  // If active is ALL/ALL4, all questions apply
  if (activeSet.indexOf('ALL') >= 0 || activeSet.indexOf('ALL4') >= 0) return true;

  for (var i = 0; i < qSet.length; i++) {
    if (activeSet.indexOf(qSet[i]) >= 0) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex(currentIdx) {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var qs = st.questions || [];
  var answers = st.answers || {};

  function dimOf(q) {
    return String(q.dimension || q.Dimension_ID || q.Dimension || q.DimensionId || '').trim();
  }
  function idOf(q) {
    return String(q.id || '').trim();
  }

  var pathway = st.pathway || st.pathwayMode || 'AWD';

  var i = Number(currentIdx || 0) + 1;
  while (i < qs.length) {
    var q = qs[i];
    if (!q) { i++; continue; }

    var qid = idOf(q);
    var dim = dimOf(q);

    // (A) Skip already skipped questions
    if (qid && answers[qid] && answers[qid].skipped) { i++; continue; }

    // (B) Skip questions not applicable to current pathway set (if helper exists)
    if (typeof advQuestionApplicableToCurrentPathwaySet === 'function') {
      if (!advQuestionApplicableToCurrentPathwaySet(q)) {
        i++;
        continue;
      }
    }

    // (C) If the dimension is gated/deferred => jump past the WHOLE dimension
    if (dim && typeof advCheckDimensionEntryGate === 'function') {
      var gated = advCheckDimensionEntryGate(dim, pathway);
      if (gated) {
        var skipDim = dim;
        while (i < qs.length) {
          var d2 = dimOf(qs[i]);
          if (d2 !== skipDim) break;
          i++;
        }
        continue; // re-evaluate at first question of next dimension
      }
    }

    // Found eligible question
    return i;
  }

  return -1;
}


        /**
         * Go to next question
         */
        function advNextQuestion() {
            var idx = ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex;

            // Handle probe answer — probe mode bypasses the normal question lookup
            if (ADVANCED.state.activeProbeItem) {
                var probeItem = ADVANCED.state.activeProbeItem;
                ADVANCED.state.activeProbeItem = null;
                if (!ADVANCED.state.probeUses) ADVANCED.state.probeUses = {};
                ADVANCED.state.probeUses[probeItem.probeId] = (ADVANCED.state.probeUses[probeItem.probeId] || 0) + 1;
                var probe = probeItem.probe;
                var sigKey = String(probe.Signal_Key || '');
                var confBoost = parseFloat(probe.Confidence_Boost || 0.3) || 0.3;
                if (ADVANCED.state.signals && ADVANCED.state.signals[sigKey]) {
                    var sig = ADVANCED.state.signals[sigKey];
                    sig.confidence = Math.min(1.0, parseFloat(sig.confidence || 0) + confBoost);
                    console.log('[Advanced] Probe answered:', probeItem.probeId, '→ boosted', sigKey, 'confidence to', sig.confidence);
                }
                advRenderCurrentQuestion();
                return;
            }

            var q = ADVANCED.state.questions[idx];

            var answer = null;
            var qType = String(q.type || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            if (qType === 'numeric') { qType = 'numeric_direct'; }
            if (qType === 'yes_no' || qType === 'boolean' || qType === 'yes_no_conditional') { qType = 'yes_no_explain'; }
            if (qType === 'multi_select' || qType === 'multi_choice') { qType = 'categorical_direct'; }
            if (qType === 'single_choice' || qType === 'single_select_direct' || qType === 'categorical') { qType = 'categorical_direct'; }
            if (qType === 'text' || qType === 'open_text' || qType === 'free_text') { qType = 'text_input'; }
            if (!qType && q.options && q.options.length > 0) { qType = 'categorical_direct'; }
            if (!qType) { qType = 'yes_no_explain'; }

            if (qType.indexOf('single') >= 0 || qType === 'categorical_direct' || qType === 'yes_no_conditional') {
                var selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="adv_q_${q.id}"]:checked`);
                if (selected) {
                    answer = {
                        questionId: q.id,
                        dimension: q.dimension,
                        answerCode: selected.value,
                        answerLabel: selected.parentElement.querySelector('.adv-option-label').textContent,
                        score: Number(selected.getAttribute('data-score')) || 0,
                        confidence: Number(selected.getAttribute('data-confidence')) || 0.85,
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }
            } else if (qType === 'number' || qType === 'numeric_direct') {
                var input = document.querySelector('.adv-numeric-input');
                if (input && input.value) {
                    answer = {
                        questionId: q.id,
                        dimension: q.dimension,
                        answerValue: parseFloat(input.value),
                        unit: q.unit || '',
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }
            } else if (qType === 'yes_no_explain') {
                var selected = document.querySelector('input[name="adv_q_' + q.id + '"]:checked');
                if (selected) {
                    var explainEl = document.getElementById('adv_explain_' + q.id);
                    answer = {
                        questionId: q.id,
                        dimension: q.dimension,
                        answerCode: selected.value,
                        answerLabel: selected.parentElement.querySelector('.adv-option-label').textContent,
                        notes: explainEl ? explainEl.value.trim() : '',
                        score: Number(selected.getAttribute('data-score')) || 0,
                        confidence: Number(selected.getAttribute('data-confidence')) || 0.85,
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }
            } else if (qType === 'text_input') {
                var textEl = document.querySelector('.adv-numeric-input');
                if (textEl && textEl.value && textEl.value.trim()) {
                    answer = {
                        questionId: q.id,
                        dimension: q.dimension,
                        answerValue: textEl.value.trim(),
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }
            } else {
                // Fallback: read radio (same as yes_no_explain — the fallback renderer produces radios)
                var selFb = document.querySelector('input[name="adv_q_' + q.id + '"]:checked');
                if (selFb) {
                    answer = {
                        questionId: q.id,
                        dimension: q.dimension,
                        answerCode: selFb.value,
                        answerLabel: (selFb.parentElement.querySelector('.adv-option-label') || {}).textContent || selFb.value,
                        score: Number(selFb.getAttribute('data-score')) || 0,
                        confidence: Number(selFb.getAttribute('data-confidence')) || 0.85,
                        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }
            }

            if (!answer) {
                alert('Please provide an answer');
                return;
            }

            ADVANCED.state.answers[q.id] = answer;
            console.log('[Advanced] Saved answer:', answer);

            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').innerHTML = '<span>Saving...</span>';

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
                    if (result.ok) {
                        console.log('[Advanced] Saved to sheet:', result.responseId);
                        try {

                        // Step 1a: Option-level signal derivation (Derives_Signal_Key/Value in options sheet).
                        // Runs before derivation rules so signals are ready for rule evaluation.
                        if (answer.answerCode && q.options) {
                            for (var oi = 0; oi < q.options.length; oi++) {
                                if (q.options[oi].code === answer.answerCode) {
                                    var selOpt = q.options[oi];
                                    if (selOpt.derivesSignalKey) {
                                        ADVANCED.state.signals[selOpt.derivesSignalKey] = {
                                            value: String(selOpt.derivesSignalValue || ''),
                                            score: answer.score || 0
                                        };
                                        console.log('[Advanced] Option signal:', selOpt.derivesSignalKey, '=', selOpt.derivesSignalValue);
                                    }
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Step 1b: Apply derivation rules to emit additional signals
                        advApplyDerivationRules(answer.questionId, answer.answerCode || '');

                        // Step 1c: Re-evaluate composite rules after new signals are available
                        advApplyCompositeRules();

                        // Step 1d: Check probe templates — queue confidence-based follow-up probes
                        advCheckAndTriggerProbes(answer.questionId);

                        // Step 2: Apply inference rules (skip questions + emit signals based on derived signals)
                        advCheckInferenceDisqualifiers(answer);
                        // Re-evaluate composite rules in case inference emitted new signals
                        advApplyCompositeRules();

                        // Step 3: Apply routing rules BEFORE checkpoint: a routing jump (SKIP_TO / ASK)
                        // may cross dimension boundaries intentionally, so the checkpoint must
                        // not intercept it. Checkpoint only fires on plain linear progression.
                        var routing = advApplyRoutingRules(answer.questionId);
                        if (routing) {
                            console.log('[Advanced] Routing rule fired from', answer.questionId, '→ action:', routing.action, 'nextId:', routing.nextId, 'currentIdx before:', ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex);
                            if (routing.action === 'END') {
                                advShowResults();
                                return;
                            }
                            if (routing.nextId) {
                                var idxMap = ADVANCED.state.questionIndexById || {};
                                var routeNextId = String(routing.nextId || '').trim();
                                var targetIdx = (routeNextId in idxMap) ? idxMap[routeNextId] : -1;
                                if (targetIdx >= 0) {
                                    if (targetIdx < ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex) {
                                        console.warn('[Advanced] ⚠️ BACKWARD routing! from idx', ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex, '→ idx', targetIdx, '(', routing.nextId, ')');
                                        ADVANCED.state._lastRenderedIdx = targetIdx; // suppress false duplicate warning
                                    }
                                    ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex = targetIdx;
                                    advRenderCurrentQuestion();
                                    return;
                                }
                                console.warn('[Advanced] Routing target not found:', routeNextId, '— falling through to next');
                            }
                        }

                        // Step 3b: Option-level follow-up injection (only when no routing rule fired).
                        // If the selected option specifies Follow_Up_Question_ID, jump to it directly.
                        if (answer.answerCode && q.options) {
                            for (var oi2 = 0; oi2 < q.options.length; oi2++) {
                                if (q.options[oi2].code === answer.answerCode) {
                                    var selOpt2 = q.options[oi2];
                                    if (selOpt2.triggersFollowUp && selOpt2.followUpQuestionId) {
                                        var fuMap = ADVANCED.state.questionIndexById || {};
                                        var followUpId = String(selOpt2.followUpQuestionId || '').trim();
                                        var fuIdx = (followUpId in fuMap) ? fuMap[followUpId] : -1;
                                        if (fuIdx >= 0) {
                                            ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex = fuIdx;
                                            advRenderCurrentQuestion();
                                            return;
                                        }
                                        console.warn('[Advanced] Follow-up question not found:', followUpId, '— falling through to next');;
                                    }
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Step 3c: Queue any summary checkpoint triggered by this question.
                        // Use trimmed q.id to guard against trailing-whitespace mismatches from Google Sheets.
                        // De-duplicate: don't queue the same checkpoint more than once (e.g., if user edits and re-answers the trigger question).
                        var summChkList = (ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointsByQuestion || {})[String(q.id || '').trim()] || [];
                        summChkList.forEach(function(chk) {
                            if (!ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointQueue) ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointQueue = [];
                            var chkId = String(chk.Checkpoint_ID || '');
                            var alreadyQueued = ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointQueue.some(function(c) { return String(c.Checkpoint_ID || '') === chkId; });
                            if (!alreadyQueued) ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointQueue.push(chk);
                        });

                        // Step 4: No routing rule fired → linear progression.
                        // Show dimension checkpoint if the next sequential question starts a new dimension.
                        // Step 4: No routing rule fired → move to next ELIGIBLE question (not blindly idx+1).
// Keep existing checkpoint behavior first (it is based on sequential transition logic in your current code).
if (advCheckCheckpoint(idx)) {
    return;
}

var nextEligibleIdx = advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex(ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex);

if (nextEligibleIdx < 0) {
    console.log('[Advanced] No more eligible questions after idx', ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex, '→ showing results');
    advShowResults();
    return;
}

ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex = nextEligibleIdx;
advRenderCurrentQuestion();
                        } catch (pipelineErr) {
                            console.error('[Advanced] Pipeline error after save:', pipelineErr);
                            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                            document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').innerHTML = '<span>Next</span><span>→</span>';
                        }
                    } else {
                        alert('Error saving answer: ' + result.error);
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').innerHTML = '<span>Next</span><span>→</span>';
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
                    alert('Failed to save: ' + error.message);
                    document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                    document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').innerHTML = '<span>Next</span><span>→</span>';
                })
                .saveAdvancedResponse(ADVANCED.state.sessionId, q.id, answer);
        }

        /**
         * Go back
         */
        function advGoBack() {
            if (ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex > 0) {
                ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex--;
                advRenderCurrentQuestion();
            }
        }

 function advSkipQuestion() {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var idx = Number(st.currentQuestionIndex || 0);
  var q = (st.questions || [])[idx];

  // Mark current question as skipped (dimension-aware)
  if (q && q.id) {
    if (typeof advMarkSkipped === 'function') {
      advMarkSkipped(q.id, 'user_skip');
    } else {
      st.answers[q.id] = { questionId: q.id, answerCode: '__SKIPPED__', skipped: true, reason: 'user_skip', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
    }
    console.log('[Advanced] User skipped question:', q.id);
  }

  // Move to next eligible question (NOT idx+1)
  if (typeof advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex === 'function') {
    var nextIdx = advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex(idx);
    if (nextIdx >= 0) {
      st.currentQuestionIndex = nextIdx;
      advRenderCurrentQuestion();
      return;
    }
  }

  // Fallback last resort
  st.currentQuestionIndex = idx + 1;
  advRenderCurrentQuestion();
}

        /**
         * Check disqualifiers — delegates to advCheckInferenceDisqualifiers (sheet rules + hardcoded fallback)
         */
        function advCheckDisqualifiers(answer) {
            var reason = advCheckInferenceDisqualifiers(answer);
            if (reason) {
                advShowDisqualification(reason);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

 /**
 * Check checkpoint (dimension boundary)
 * Uses advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex() so it respects:
 * - inference/user/routing skips
 * - pathway applicability (if helper exists)
 * - dimension entry-gates (including deferrals)
 */
function advCheckCheckpoint(currentIdx) {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var questions = st.questions || [];
  if (!questions.length) return false;

  var curIdx = Number(currentIdx || 0);
  var curQ = questions[curIdx];
  if (!curQ) return false;

  function dimOf(q) {
    return String(q.dimension || q.Dimension_ID || q.Dimension || q.DimensionId || '').trim();
  }

  // Find next eligible question (this already handles gates + skips correctly)
  var nextIdx = (typeof advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex === 'function')
    ? advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex(curIdx)
    : (curIdx + 1);

  if (nextIdx == null || nextIdx < 0 || nextIdx >= questions.length) return false;

  var nextQ = questions[nextIdx];
  if (!nextQ) return false;

  var curDim = dimOf(curQ);
  var nextDim = dimOf(nextQ);

  // If dimension IDs missing, don't block the flow with a checkpoint
  if (!curDim || !nextDim) return false;

  if (curDim !== nextDim) {
    // Store where to resume after user clicks Continue
    st.pendingCheckpointNextIndex = nextIdx;
    st.pendingCheckpointFromDim = curDim;
    st.pendingCheckpointToDim = nextDim;

    console.log('[Advanced][Checkpoint] Boundary detected', {
      fromDim: curDim,
      toDim: nextDim,
      fromIdx: curIdx,
      nextIdx: nextIdx,
      fromQ: curQ.id,
      toQ: nextQ.id
    });

    // Show summary for the dimension we just finished
    advShowCheckpoint(curDim);
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

        /**
         * Show checkpoint
         */
        function advShowCheckpoint(dimension) {
    var lang = CPOSL.language || 'EN';
    var answers = ADVANCED.state.answers || {};

    // Normalize dimension id
    var fromDim = String(dimension || '').trim();
    if (!fromDim) fromDim = '—';

    // OPTIONAL: show the upcoming dimension (set by advCheckCheckpoint)
    var toDim = String(ADVANCED.state.pendingCheckpointToDim || '').trim();

    // Use CPOS_Advanced_Dimension_Order data if available, fall back to hardcoded map.
    var dimFlowCfg = (ADVANCED.state.dimFlowById || {})[fromDim];
    var hardcodedDimNames = {
        'D1': { en: 'Biophysical Suitability',    hi: 'जैवभौतिक उपयुक्तता',       icon: '🌾' },
        'D2': { en: 'Infrastructure & Access',    hi: 'बुनियादी ढांचा',         icon: '💧' },
        'D3': { en: 'Location & Context',         hi: 'स्थान',                  icon: '📍' },
        'D4': { en: 'Legal & Carbon Eligibility', hi: 'कानूनी पात्रता',         icon: '📜' },
        'D5': { en: 'Economic Viability',         hi: 'आर्थिक व्यवहार्यता',     icon: '💰' },
        'D6': { en: 'Carbon Potential',           hi: 'कार्बन क्षमता',          icon: '🌍' },
        'D7': { en: 'Farm Management',            hi: 'खेत प्रबंधन',            icon: '🌱' },
        'D8': { en: 'Material Handling',          hi: 'सामग्री प्रबंधन',        icon: '🪨' },
        'D10': { en: 'Biomass & Biochar Readiness', hi: 'बायोचार तैयारी',      icon: '🔥' },
        'I1': { en: 'Human Capital & Capacity',   hi: 'मानव क्षमता',            icon: '👤' },
        'I2': { en: 'Resources & Labor',          hi: 'संसाधन और श्रम',         icon: '🔧' },
        'I3': { en: 'Financial Capacity',         hi: 'वित्तीय क्षमता',         icon: '💼' },
        'I4': { en: 'Risk Management',            hi: 'जोखिम प्रबंधन',          icon: '🛡️' },
        'I5': { en: 'Support & Extension',        hi: 'सहायता और विस्तार',      icon: '🤝' }
    };

    var dimInfo;
    if (dimFlowCfg) {
        var labelEN = dimFlowCfg.Stage_Label || dimFlowCfg.Dimension_Name || dimFlowCfg.DimensionName || fromDim;
        dimInfo = {
            en: labelEN,
            hi: (hardcodedDimNames[fromDim] || {}).hi || labelEN,
            icon: dimFlowCfg.Icon || dimFlowCfg.icon || (hardcodedDimNames[fromDim] || {}).icon || '📋'
        };
    } else {
        dimInfo = hardcodedDimNames[fromDim];
    }
    if (!dimInfo) {
        dimInfo = { en: 'Section ' + fromDim, hi: 'अनुभाग ' + fromDim, icon: '📋' };
    }

    // Next dimension label (optional)
    var nextDimCfg = toDim ? (ADVANCED.state.dimFlowById || {})[toDim] : null;
    var nextDimLabelEN = nextDimCfg ? (nextDimCfg.Stage_Label || nextDimCfg.Dimension_Name || toDim) : (hardcodedDimNames[toDim] ? hardcodedDimNames[toDim].en : toDim);
    var nextDimLabelHI = nextDimCfg ? (nextDimCfg.Stage_Label_HI || nextDimCfg.Stage_Label || nextDimCfg.Dimension_Name || toDim) : (hardcodedDimNames[toDim] ? hardcodedDimNames[toDim].hi : toDim);

    var title = (lang === 'HI') ? dimInfo.hi : dimInfo.en;
    var icon = dimInfo.icon;

    // ✅ Only include real (non-skipped) answers for this dimension
    var dimAnswers = Object.values(answers).filter(function(a) {
        if (!a) return false;
        if (a.skipped || a === '__SKIPPED__') return false;
        if (String(a.answerCode || '').trim() === '__SKIPPED__') return false;
        return String(a.dimension || '').trim() === fromDim;
    });

    var statsHTML = '';
    var totalScore = 0;
    var count = 0;

    dimAnswers.forEach(function(ans) {
        var label = (ans.answerLabel != null && ans.answerLabel !== '') ? ans.answerLabel
                  : (ans.answerValue != null && ans.answerValue !== '') ? ans.answerValue
                  : '—';

        statsHTML += `
          <div class="adv-summary-stat">
            <div class="label">${escapeHtml(ans.questionId || '')}</div>
            <div class="value">${escapeHtml(String(label).substring(0, 30))}</div>
            <div class="icon">✓</div>
          </div>
        `;

        if (ans.score !== undefined && ans.score !== null && ans.score !== '') {
            totalScore += Number(ans.score) || 0;
            count++;
        }
    });

    var avgScore = count > 0 ? Math.round(totalScore / count) : 0;
    var scoreLabel = lang === 'HI' ? 'स्कोर' : 'Score';
    var suitLabel = lang === 'HI' ? 'उपयुक्तता' : 'suitability';
    var continueBtn = lang === 'HI' ? 'आगे बढ़ें →' : 'Continue →';

    // Stage_Goal from CPOS_Advanced_Dimension_Order — show as subtitle if available
    var stageGoal = dimFlowCfg ? String(dimFlowCfg.Stage_Goal || dimFlowCfg.StageGoal || '') : '';

    var nextLine = '';
    if (toDim) {
        var nextTxt = (lang === 'HI') ? nextDimLabelHI : nextDimLabelEN;
        nextLine = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);margin:6px 0 0 0;">
                      Next: <b>${escapeHtml(nextTxt || toDim)}</b>
                    </div>`;
    }

    var cardHTML = `
      <div class="adv-checkpoint-card">
        <div class="adv-checkpoint-header">
          <div class="adv-checkpoint-icon">${icon}</div>
          <div class="adv-checkpoint-title">${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
        </div>
        ${stageGoal ? `<div style="font-size:13px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);margin:-4px 0 8px 0;padding:0 4px;">${escapeHtml(stageGoal)}</div>` : ''}
        ${nextLine}
        <div class="adv-summary-grid">${statsHTML}</div>
        <div class="adv-confidence-bar">
          <div class="label">${scoreLabel}</div>
          <div class="bar"><div class="fill" style="width:${avgScore}%"></div></div>
          <div class="text">${avgScore}% ${suitLabel}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="adv-checkpoint-actions">
          <button class="btn primary" onclick="advConfirmCheckpoint()">${continueBtn}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    var questionCard = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
    if (questionCard) questionCard.innerHTML = cardHTML;
    var questionContext = document.getElementById('advQuestionContext');
    if (questionContext) questionContext.style.display = 'none';
    var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
    if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';

    var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
    if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'none';
    var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
    if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'none';
    var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
    if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'none';
}

function advConfirmCheckpoint() {
  var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
  if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'inline-flex';
  var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
  if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'inline-flex';
  var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
  if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'inline-flex';

  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var idx = Number(st.currentQuestionIndex || 0);

  // Preferred: jump to the next eligible index computed during checkpoint detection
  if (typeof st.pendingCheckpointNextIndex === 'number' && st.pendingCheckpointNextIndex >= 0) {
    st.currentQuestionIndex = st.pendingCheckpointNextIndex;
    st.pendingCheckpointNextIndex = null;
    st.pendingCheckpointFromDim = null;
    st.pendingCheckpointToDim = null;
    advRenderCurrentQuestion();
    return;
  }

  // Fallback: compute next eligible now
  if (typeof advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex === 'function') {
    var nextIdx = advFindNextEligibleQuestionIndex(idx);
    if (nextIdx >= 0) {
      st.currentQuestionIndex = nextIdx;
      advRenderCurrentQuestion();
      return;
    }
  }

  // Last resort fallback
  st.currentQuestionIndex = idx + 1;
  advRenderCurrentQuestion();
}

        /**
         * Resolve summary checkpoint template placeholders.
         * - {ADV_XX_XXX_LABEL} → answer label for that question
         * - {SIGNAL_KEY}       → signal value from ADVANCED.state.signals
         * - {SOC_SUMMARY}      → composite of ADV_D1_004 + ADV_D1_005 answers
         * - **text**           → <strong>text</strong>
         * - \n (literal or actual) → <br>
         */
        function advResolveSummaryTemplate(template) {
            if (!template) return '';
            var signals = ADVANCED.state.signals || {};
            var answers = ADVANCED.state.answers || {};

            // {ADV_XX_XXX_LABEL} → answer label
            template = template.replace(/\{(ADV_[A-Z0-9_]+)_LABEL\}/g, function(match, qId) {
                var ans = answers[qId];
                if (!ans || ans === '__SKIPPED__' || (ans && ans.skipped)) return '<em style="opacity:0.6">—</em>';
                return escapeHtml(String(ans.answerLabel || ans.answerCode || ans.answerValue || '—'));
            });

            // {SOC_SUMMARY} — special composite value
            template = template.replace(/\{SOC_SUMMARY\}/g, function() {
                var soc4 = answers['ADV_D1_004'];
                var soc5 = answers['ADV_D1_005'];
                var parts = [];
                if (soc4) parts.push(soc4.answerLabel || soc4.answerCode || String(soc4.answerValue || ''));
                if (soc5) parts.push(soc5.answerLabel || soc5.answerCode || String(soc5.answerValue || ''));
                return escapeHtml(parts.join(' / ') || '—');
            });

            // {SIGNAL_KEY} → signal value
            template = template.replace(/\{([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*)\}/g, function(match, key) {
                if (signals[key]) return escapeHtml(String(signals[key].value || '—'));
                return '<em style="opacity:0.6">—</em>';
            });

            // Markdown bold
            template = template.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');

            // Newlines (literal \n or actual newline)
            template = template.replace(/\\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n/g, '<br>');

            return template;
        }

        /**
         * Show a summary checkpoint card.
         * Called from advRenderCurrentQuestion when a checkpoint is dequeued.
         */
        function advShowSummaryCheckpoint(checkpoint) {
            var lang = CPOSL.language || 'EN';
            var templateRaw = (lang === 'HI' && checkpoint.Summary_Template_HI)
                ? checkpoint.Summary_Template_HI : checkpoint.Summary_Template_EN;
            var resolvedHtml = advResolveSummaryTemplate(templateRaw);

            var confirmRequired = String(checkpoint.Confirmation_Required || 'TRUE').toUpperCase() !== 'FALSE';
            var allowEdit = String(checkpoint.Allow_Edit || 'TRUE').toUpperCase() !== 'FALSE';
            var nextActionIfNo = String(checkpoint.Next_Action_If_No || '').trim();
            var groupLabel = escapeHtml(String(checkpoint.Dimension_Group || 'Summary'));

            var yesLabel = lang === 'HI' ? '✓ हाँ, सही है' : '✓ Yes, this is accurate';
            var noLabel  = lang === 'HI' ? '✗ नहीं, वापस जाएं' : '✗ No, let me go back and edit';
            var contLabel = lang === 'HI' ? 'आगे बढ़ें →' : 'Continue →';

            var actionsHTML = '<button class="btn primary" onclick="advSummaryConfirmed()" style="margin:4px 0;">' +
                (confirmRequired ? yesLabel : contLabel) + '</button>';
            if (confirmRequired && allowEdit && nextActionIfNo) {
                // Use single-quoted string to avoid breaking the double-quoted onclick HTML attribute
                var safeAction = nextActionIfNo.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
                actionsHTML += '<button class="btn secondary" onclick="advSummaryDenied(\'' +
                    safeAction + '\')" style="margin:4px 0;">' + noLabel + '</button>';
            }
            // Track the trigger question index so advSummaryConfirmed can guarantee forward progress
            ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryTriggerIdx = ADVANCED.state.questionIndexById[
                String(checkpoint.Trigger_After_Question || '').trim()
            ];
            console.log('[Advanced] advShowSummaryCheckpoint:', checkpoint.Checkpoint_ID,
                'trigger:', checkpoint.Trigger_After_Question,
                'trigIdx:', ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryTriggerIdx,
                'currentIdx:', ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex,
                'nextActionIfNo:', nextActionIfNo);

            var cardHTML = '<div class="adv-checkpoint-card">' +
                '<div class="adv-checkpoint-header">' +
                  '<div class="adv-checkpoint-icon">📋</div>' +
                  '<div class="adv-checkpoint-title">' + groupLabel + ' Summary</div>' +
                '</div>' +
                '<div style="padding:14px 0;line-height:1.75;font-size:14px;color:#374151;">' + resolvedHtml + '</div>' +
                '<div class="adv-checkpoint-actions">' + actionsHTML + '</div>' +
                '</div>';

            var questionCard = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
            if (questionCard) questionCard.innerHTML = cardHTML;
            var questionContext = document.getElementById('advQuestionContext');
            if (questionContext) questionContext.style.display = 'none';
            var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
            if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';
            var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
            if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'none';
            var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
            if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'none';
            var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
            if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'none';
        }

        /** Farmer confirmed summary — advance to next question */
        function advSummaryConfirmed() {
            // Guarantee forward progress using two anchors:
            // 1. pendingSummaryTriggerIdx: index of the question that triggered this summary
            //    (via questionIndexById lookup — may be undefined if ID had trim mismatch)
            // 2. pendingSummaryShowIdx: currentQuestionIndex at the moment the summary was shown
            //    (always defined — set just before the call in advRenderCurrentQuestion)
            var trigIdx = ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryTriggerIdx;
            var showIdx = ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryShowIdx;
            var minIdx = (trigIdx !== undefined) ? trigIdx : showIdx;
            console.log('[Advanced] advSummaryConfirmed: trigIdx=' + trigIdx + ' showIdx=' + showIdx + ' minIdx=' + minIdx + ' currentIdx=' + ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex);
            if (minIdx !== undefined && ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex <= minIdx) {
                ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex = minIdx + 1;
                console.log('[Advanced] Summary confirmed: bumped currentQuestionIndex to', ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex);
            }
            ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryTriggerIdx = undefined;
            ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryShowIdx = undefined;
            var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
            if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
            if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
            if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            advRenderCurrentQuestion();
        }

        /** Farmer denied summary — navigate back to specified question */
        function advSummaryDenied(nextAction) {
            ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryTriggerIdx = undefined;
            ADVANCED.state.pendingSummaryShowIdx = undefined;
            var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
            if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
            if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
            if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            console.log('[Advanced] advSummaryDenied: nextAction=' + nextAction + ' currentIdx=' + ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex);
            if (nextAction && nextAction.indexOf('GO_BACK_TO_') === 0) {
                var targetId = nextAction.replace('GO_BACK_TO_', '');
                var idxMap = ADVANCED.state.questionIndexById || {};
                var targetIdx = (targetId in idxMap) ? idxMap[targetId] : -1;
                if (targetIdx >= 0) {
                    ADVANCED.state.currentQuestionIndex = targetIdx;
                    ADVANCED.state._lastRenderedIdx = targetIdx; // intentional backward nav — suppress false warning
                    // Clear the summary / probe queues
                    ADVANCED.state.summaryCheckpointQueue = [];
                    ADVANCED.state.probeQueue = [];
                    // Clear all answers for questions from targetIdx through the end of
                    // that dimension so the farmer truly re-answers from scratch.
                    // Signals will be re-derived as they answer; stale signal values
                    // from the old answers will be overwritten by the new pipeline runs.
                    var questions = ADVANCED.state.questions;
                    var targetDim = questions[targetIdx] ? questions[targetIdx].dimension : null;
                    if (targetDim) {
                        for (var ci = targetIdx; ci < questions.length; ci++) {
                            if (questions[ci].dimension !== targetDim) break;
                            delete ADVANCED.state.answers[questions[ci].id];
                        }
                    }
                    advRenderCurrentQuestion();
                    return;
                }
                console.warn('[Advanced] Summary back-target not found:', targetId);
            }
            advRenderCurrentQuestion();
        }

        /**
         * Show disqualification
         */
        function advShowDisqualification(reason) {
            var lang = CPOSL.language || 'EN';

            var messages = {
                'RAINFED': {
                    title_EN: 'AWD Not Applicable',
                    title_HI: 'AWD लागू नहीं',
                    reason_EN: 'AWD requires irrigation control. Your field is rainfed.',
                    reason_HI: 'AWD को सिंचाई नियंत्रण चाहिए। आपका खेत वर्षा आधारित है।',
                    suggestion_EN: 'Consider SRI or Biochar pathways instead.',
                    suggestion_HI: 'SRI या बायोचार मार्ग पर विचार करें।'
                },
                'SHARECROP': {
                    title_EN: 'Carbon Credits Require Ownership',
                    title_HI: 'कार्बन क्रेडिट के लिए स्वामित्व चाहिए',
                    reason_EN: 'Carbon credits require land ownership. Sharecropping does not qualify.',
                    reason_HI: 'कार्बन क्रेडिट के लिए भूमि स्वामित्व चाहिए। बटाई योग्य नहीं।',
                    suggestion_EN: 'AWD can still save water. Consider without carbon credits.',
                    suggestion_HI: 'AWD पानी बचा सकता है। कार्बन क्रेडिट बिना विचार करें।'
                },
                'ALREADY_AWD': {
                    title_EN: 'Already Practicing AWD',
                    title_HI: 'पहले से AWD',
                    reason_EN: 'You already practice AWD. Carbon credits need additionality.',
                    reason_HI: 'आप पहले से AWD करते हैं। कार्बन क्रेडिट को अतिरिक्तता चाहिए।',
                    suggestion_EN: 'Great! Consider Biochar or ERW pathways.',
                    suggestion_HI: 'बढ़िया! बायोचार या ERW देखें।'
                }
            };

            var msg = messages[reason] || {
                title_EN: 'Not Eligible for This Pathway',
                title_HI: 'इस पाथवे के लिए पात्र नहीं',
                reason_EN: reason.replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, function(c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }),
                reason_HI: reason.replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, function(c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }),
                suggestion_EN: 'Please speak with your field officer for more information.',
                suggestion_HI: 'अधिक जानकारी के लिए अपने फील्ड अधिकारी से बात करें।'
            };

            var title = lang === 'HI' ? msg.title_HI : msg.title_EN;
            var reasonText = lang === 'HI' ? msg.reason_HI : msg.reason_EN;
            var suggestion = lang === 'HI' ? msg.suggestion_HI : msg.suggestion_EN;
            var altLabel = lang === 'HI' ? 'विकल्प' : 'Alternative';
            var btnLabel = lang === 'HI' ? 'नया शुरू करें' : 'Start New';

            var alertHTML = `
    <div class="adv-disqualification">
      <div class="header">
        <div class="icon">🚫</div>
        <div class="title">${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="reason">${escapeHtml(reasonText)}</div>
      <div class="suggestion">
        <div class="suggestion-title">💡 ${altLabel}</div>
        <div class="suggestion-text">${escapeHtml(suggestion)}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="margin-top:24px;text-align:center;">
        <button class="btn primary" onclick="restartAdvancedAssessment()">🔄 ${btnLabel}</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

            var questionCard = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
            if (questionCard) questionCard.innerHTML = alertHTML;
            var questionContext = document.getElementById('advQuestionContext');
            if (questionContext) questionContext.style.display = 'none';
            var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
            if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';
            var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
            if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'none';
            var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
            if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'none';
            var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
            if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'none';

            ADVANCED.state.active = false;
        }

        /**
         * Restart
         */
        function restartAdvancedAssessment() {
             var advancedInterface = document.getElementById('advancedInterface');
            if (advancedInterface) advancedInterface.style.display = 'none';
            var advancedResults = document.getElementById('advancedResults');
            if (advancedResults) advancedResults.style.display = 'none';
            var advancedWelcome = document.getElementById('advancedWelcome');
            if (advancedWelcome) advancedWelcome.style.display = 'block';
        }

  function advParseCompositeConditions(str) {
  var conditions = [];
  if (!str) return conditions;

  // Split on commas outside parentheses
  var parts = [], depth = 0, cur = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    var c = str[i];
    if (c === '(') { depth++; cur += c; }
    else if (c === ')') { depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1); cur += c; }
    else if (c === ',' && depth === 0) { parts.push(cur.trim()); cur = ''; }
    else { cur += c; }
  }
  if (cur.trim()) parts.push(cur.trim());
