  // Accept signal keys like ABC_DEF_123 (underscore/digits) and also hyphen if ever used
  // Parse:
  //   SIG=VAL
  //   SIG != VAL
  //   SIG IN (A,B|C)
  //   SIG NOT_EQUALS VAL
  //   SIG EQUALS VAL
  parts.forEach(function(part) {
    if (!part) return;
    part = String(part).trim();
    if (!part) return;

    // IN (...)
    var inM = part.match(/^([A-Za-z0-9_\-]+)\s+IN\s*\(([^)]+)\)\s*$/i);
    if (inM) {
      var raw = String(inM[2] || '');
      var vals = raw.split(/[|,]/).map(function(v){ return String(v||'').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      conditions.push({ signal: inM[1], op: 'IN', values: vals });
      return;
    }

    // NOT_EQUALS / !=
    var neqM = part.match(/^([A-Za-z0-9_\-]+)\s*(?:!=|NOT_EQUALS)\s*(.+)$/i);
    if (neqM) {
      conditions.push({ signal: neqM[1], op: '!=', value: String(neqM[2] || '').trim() });
      return;
    }

    // EQUALS / =
    var eqM = part.match(/^([A-Za-z0-9_\-]+)\s*(?:=|EQUALS)\s*(.+)$/i);
    if (eqM) {
      conditions.push({ signal: eqM[1], op: '=', value: String(eqM[2] || '').trim() });
      return;
    }

    // If no match, ignore (keeps parser fail-safe)
  });

  return conditions;
}

        function advApplyCompositeRules() {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var rules = (st.compositeRules || []).slice().sort(function(a, b) {
    return (parseFloat(a.Priority || 99)) - (parseFloat(b.Priority || 99));
  });
  var signals = st.signals || (st.signals = {});
  var fired = 0;

  function norm(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toUpperCase(); }

  function readSignalValue(sigObj) {
    if (sigObj == null) return '';
    if (typeof sigObj === 'object' && sigObj !== null && ('value' in sigObj)) {
      return String(sigObj.value == null ? '' : sigObj.value).trim();
    }
    return String(sigObj).trim();
  }

  function getSigVal(key) {
    return readSignalValue(signals[key]);
  }

  // Basic categorical → numeric map (used for WEIGHTED)
  function scoreFromCategorical(val) {
    var v = norm(val);

    // readiness-like
    if (v === 'READY' || v === 'ELIGIBLE' || v === 'ACCESSIBLE' || v === 'HIGH_POTENTIAL' || v === 'HIGH_POTENTIAL_CARBON_GRADE') return 100;
    if (v === 'PARTIAL' || v === 'CONDITIONAL' || v === 'SUPPORTED_FIT' || v === 'MODERATE_SUITABILITY' || v === 'PROGRAM_FEASIBLE' || v === 'MRV_READY_OR_SUPPORTABLE') return 65;
    if (v === 'CONSTRAINED' || v === 'PASS_WITH_FLAGS' || v === 'MIXED_BASE' || v === 'MODERATE') return 50;
    if (v === 'NOT_READY' || v === 'NOT_ELIGIBLE' || v === 'NOT_FEASIBLE' || v === 'FAIL' || v === 'LOW_READINESS' || v === 'LOW' || v === 'ISOLATED') return 10;

    // support is inverse (HIGH_SUPPORT means low readiness)
    if (v === 'HIGH_SUPPORT') return 30;
    if (v === 'LOW_SUPPORT') return 80;

    // If numeric already
    var n = parseFloat(String(val).trim());
    if (!isNaN(n)) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n));

    return 0;
  }

  function shouldWrite(sigKey, rulePriority) {
    var existing = signals[sigKey];
    if (!existing) return true;

    // Don't overwrite non-composite sources
    if (typeof existing === 'object' && existing !== null && existing.source && String(existing.source).indexOf('composite') !== 0) {
      return false;
    }

    // If existing composite came from a higher priority (lower number), don't overwrite
    var p = (typeof existing === 'object' && existing !== null && existing._priority != null)
      ? Number(existing._priority)
      : null;
    if (p != null && !isNaN(p) && p <= rulePriority) return false;

    return true;
  }

  rules.forEach(function(rule) {
    if (norm(rule.Is_Active || 'TRUE') === 'FALSE') return;

    var sigKey  = String(rule.Composite_Signal_Key || '').trim();
    var reqStr  = String(rule.Required_Signals || '').trim();
    var formula = norm(rule.Logic_Formula || 'ALL_TRUE');
    var outVal  = String(rule.Output_Signal_Value || '').trim();
    var conf    = parseFloat(rule.Confidence_0_1 || 0.8);
    var prio    = parseFloat(rule.Priority || 99);

    if (!sigKey || !reqStr) return;

    // ---------- WEIGHTED support ----------
    // Pattern: (0.35*TECH + 0.25*CARBON + 0.20*FINANCE + 0.20*CAPACITY)
    // Uses Required_Signals order as the source signals, with common token->index mapping.
    if (formula === 'WEIGHTED' || formula.indexOf('*') >= 0 || formula.indexOf('0.') >= 0) {
      // Parse weights
      var expr = String(rule.Logic_Formula || '').trim();
      var pairs = [];
      (expr.match(/(\d*\.?\d+)\s*\*\s*([A-Za-z_]+)/g) || []).forEach(function(m) {
        var mm = m.match(/(\d*\.?\d+)\s*\*\s*([A-Za-z_]+)/);
        if (mm) pairs.push({ w: parseFloat(mm[1]), token: norm(mm[2]) });
      });

      // Required signals list
      var reqList = reqStr.split(',').map(function(x){ return String(x||'').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);

      if (!pairs.length || !reqList.length) return;

      // token → required signal heuristic (works for your COMP_014 pattern)
      function tokenToSignal(token) {
        // Direct match
        for (var i = 0; i < reqList.length; i++) {
          if (norm(reqList[i]) === token) return reqList[i];
        }
        // Common aliases used in your notes
        if (token === 'TECH') return reqList[0] || null;
        if (token === 'CARBON') return reqList[1] || null;
        if (token === 'FINANCE') return reqList[2] || null;
        if (token === 'CAPACITY') return reqList[3] || null;
        return null;
      }

      var totalW = 0, totalScore = 0;
      for (var pi = 0; pi < pairs.length; pi++) {
        var p = pairs[pi];
        if (!p || isNaN(p.w) || p.w <= 0) continue;
        var sigName = tokenToSignal(p.token);
        if (!sigName) continue;

        var v = getSigVal(sigName);
        // If missing required signal, cannot compute yet
        if (!v) return;

        var sc = scoreFromCategorical(v);
        totalW += p.w;
        totalScore += p.w * sc;
      }

      if (totalW <= 0) return;

      var score = Math.round(totalScore / totalW);
      if (!shouldWrite(sigKey, prio)) return;

      signals[sigKey] = { value: score, confidence: conf, source: 'composite_weighted', _priority: prio };
      console.log('[Advanced] Composite signal:', sigKey, '=', score);
      fired++;
      return;
    }

    // ---------- Standard logical composites ----------
    if (!outVal) return;

    var conds = (typeof advParseCompositeConditions === 'function')
      ? advParseCompositeConditions(reqStr)
      : [];

    if (!conds.length) return;

    var missing = [];
    var trueCount = 0;

    conds.forEach(function(c) {
      var key = String(c.signal || '').trim();
      if (!key) return;

      if (!(key in signals)) {
        missing.push(key);
        return;
      }

      var sv = norm(getSigVal(key));

      if (c.op === '=') {
        if (sv === norm(c.value)) trueCount++;
        return;
      }
      if (c.op === '!=') {
        if (sv !== norm(c.value)) trueCount++;
        return;
      }
      if (c.op === 'IN') {
        var vals = (c.values || []).map(function(v){ return norm(v); }).filter(Boolean);
        if (vals.indexOf(sv) >= 0) trueCount++;
        return;
      }
    });

    // If anything missing, don't fire (but keep debug hint)
    if (missing.length) {
      // uncomment if you want noisy debug:
      // console.log('[Advanced][Composite] waiting for signals for', sigKey, 'missing:', missing.join(', '));
      return;
    }

    var satisfied = false;
    if (formula === 'ALL_TRUE') satisfied = (trueCount === conds.length);
    else if (formula === 'ANY_TRUE') satisfied = (trueCount > 0);
    else {
      var mof = formula.match(/^(\d+)_OF_(\d+)_TRUE$/);
      if (mof) {
        var n = parseInt(mof[1], 10);
        satisfied = trueCount >= n;
      } else {
        // fallback: treat as ALL_TRUE
        satisfied = (trueCount === conds.length);
      }
    }

    if (!satisfied) return;
    if (!shouldWrite(sigKey, prio)) return;

    signals[sigKey] = { value: outVal, confidence: conf, source: 'composite_rule', _priority: prio };
    console.log('[Advanced] Composite signal:', sigKey, '=', outVal);
    fired++;
  });

  if (fired) console.log('[Advanced] Composite rules: ' + fired + ' signal(s) emitted');
  return fired;
}

        /**
         * Apply CPOS_Advanced_Signal_to_LiteFactor_Map entries.
         * Translates signals accumulated during the advanced interview into Lite-questionnaire
         * variable selections so the Lite scoring engine doesn't need to re-ask those questions.
         * Columns: Signal_Key, When_Signal_Value, Then_Lite_Class, Lite_Variable_Name, Confidence_0_1,
         *          Is_Active, Mapping_Method (DIRECT), Estimate_Formula (future).
         */

        /**
         * Check probe templates and queue any that should trigger based on:
         * 1. A derived signal whose confidence is below Min_Confidence_To_Trigger
         * 2. A question just answered where Signal_Key === answered question ID
         */
        function advCheckAndTriggerProbes(questionId) {
            var templates = ADVANCED.state.probeTemplates || [];
            if (!templates.length) return;
            if (!ADVANCED.state.probeUses) ADVANCED.state.probeUses = {};
            if (!ADVANCED.state.probeQueue) ADVANCED.state.probeQueue = [];

            var signals = ADVANCED.state.signals || {};
            var answers = ADVANCED.state.answers || {};

            templates.forEach(function(probe) {
                var isActive = String(probe.Is_Active || 'TRUE').toUpperCase();
                if (isActive === 'FALSE') return;

                var probeId = String(probe.Probe_ID || '');
                if (!probeId) return;
                var sigKey = String(probe.Signal_Key || '');
                var minConf = parseFloat(probe.Min_Confidence_To_Trigger || 0.5) || 0.5;
                var maxUses = parseInt(probe.Max_Uses || 1) || 1;

                // Check usage limit
                if ((ADVANCED.state.probeUses[probeId] || 0) >= maxUses) return;
                // Already in queue?
                if (ADVANCED.state.probeQueue.some(function(p) { return p.probeId === probeId; })) return;

                var valueForPlaceholder = '';
                var shouldTrigger = false;

                // Signal-based trigger: signal exists but confidence below threshold
                if (signals[sigKey] && typeof signals[sigKey] === 'object') {
                    var sigConf = parseFloat(signals[sigKey].confidence || 1);
                    if (sigConf < minConf) {
                        shouldTrigger = true;
                        valueForPlaceholder = String(signals[sigKey].value || '');
                    }
                }
                // Question-answer trigger: Signal_Key is the answered question ID
                if (!shouldTrigger && sigKey === questionId) {
                    var rawAns = answers[questionId];
                    if (rawAns && rawAns !== '__SKIPPED__' && !(rawAns && rawAns.skipped)) {
                        valueForPlaceholder = (rawAns && typeof rawAns === 'object')
                            ? String(rawAns.answerCode || rawAns.value || '')
                            : String(rawAns);
                        shouldTrigger = true;
                    }
                }

                if (shouldTrigger) {
                    ADVANCED.state.probeQueue.push({ probeId: probeId, probe: probe, value: valueForPlaceholder });
                    console.log('[Advanced] Probe queued:', probeId, 'signal/question:', sigKey, 'value:', valueForPlaceholder);
                }
            });
        }

        /**
         * Render a probe question (confidence-boost follow-up from CPOS_Advanced_Probe_Templates).
         * The probe is shown as a FOLLOW-UP card; on submit, advNextQuestion handles the probe
         * answer via ADVANCED.state.activeProbeItem.
         */
        function advRenderProbeQuestion(probeItem) {
            var probe = probeItem.probe;
            var lang = CPOSL.language || 'EN';
            var value = probeItem.value || '';

            // Replace placeholders
            var rawText = (lang === 'HI' && probe.Probe_Text_HI)
                ? String(probe.Probe_Text_HI)
                : String(probe.Probe_Text_EN || '');
            rawText = rawText.replace(/\{VALUE\}/g, value).replace(/\{FLAG\}/g, value ? 'unusual' : '');

            ADVANCED.state.activeProbeItem = probeItem;

            // Update header
            var questionNumber = document.getElementById('advQuestionNumber');
            if (questionNumber) questionNumber.textContent = '🔍 FOLLOW-UP';
            var questionText = document.getElementById('advQuestionText');
            if (questionText) questionText.innerHTML = escapeHtml(rawText);
            var questionContext = document.getElementById('advQuestionContext');
            if (questionContext) questionContext.style.display = 'none';

            var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
            if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';

            var qType = String(probe.Answer_Type || 'yes_no_explain').toLowerCase().trim();

            // Parse Options_JSON (array or object)
            var syntheticOpts = [];
            if (probe.Options_JSON) {
                try {
                    var parsed = JSON.parse(probe.Options_JSON);
                    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
                        syntheticOpts = parsed.map(function(item, idx) {
                            if (typeof item === 'string') {
                                var parts = item.split('|');
                                return { code: 'OPT_' + idx, label_EN: parts[0], label_HI: parts[1] || parts[0] };
                            }
                            return item;
                        });
                    } else if (typeof parsed === 'object') {
                        // breakdown type: object of {fieldName: unit}
                        Object.keys(parsed).forEach(function(fieldKey) {
                            var unit = parsed[fieldKey] || '';
                            var row = document.createElement('div');
                            row.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px;';
                            row.innerHTML = '<label style="flex:1;color:#fff;font-size:14px;">' + escapeHtml(fieldKey) + '</label>' +
                                '<input type="number" id="probe_field_' + escapeHtml(fieldKey) + '" min="0" ' +
                                'style="width:120px;padding:8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);color:#fff;font-size:14px;" placeholder="0">' +
                                '<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.6);font-size:13px;">' + escapeHtml(unit) + '</span>';
                            answerBox.appendChild(row);
                        });
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvBack').style.display = 'none';
                        return; // early return for breakdown type
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.warn('[Advanced] Probe Options_JSON parse error:', probeItem.probeId, e); }
            }

            if ((qType === 'single_select' || qType === 'categorical_direct') && syntheticOpts.length > 0) {
                var grid = document.createElement('div');
                grid.className = 'adv-option-grid';
                syntheticOpts.forEach(function(opt) {
                    var label = (lang === 'HI' && opt.label_HI) ? opt.label_HI : opt.label_EN;
                    var div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'adv-option-item';
                    div.innerHTML = '<input type="radio" name="adv_probe_sel" value="' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" ' +
                        'id="adv_probe_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-radio">' +
                        '<label for="adv_probe_' + escapeHtml(opt.code) + '" class="adv-option-label">' + escapeHtml(label) + '</label>';
                    var radio = div.querySelector('input');
                    radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
                        grid.querySelectorAll('.adv-option-item').forEach(function(i) { i.classList.remove('selected'); });
                        div.classList.add('selected');
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                    });
                    div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') { radio.checked = true; radio.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
                    });
                    grid.appendChild(div);
                });
                answerBox.appendChild(grid);
            } else if (qType === 'number' || qType === 'numeric' || qType === 'numeric_direct') {
                var inp = document.createElement('input');
                inp.type = 'number'; inp.id = 'adv_probe_number'; inp.min = '0';
                inp.className = 'adv-numeric-input';
                inp.placeholder = lang === 'HI' ? 'यहाँ लिखें...' : 'Enter value...';
                inp.addEventListener('input', function() { document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = !inp.value; });
                answerBox.appendChild(inp);
            } else {
                // Default: Yes/No radio buttons
                var yn = document.createElement('div');
                yn.className = 'adv-option-grid';
                [{ code: 'YES', en: 'Yes', hi: 'हाँ' }, { code: 'NO', en: 'No', hi: 'नहीं' }].forEach(function(opt) {
                    var div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'adv-option-item';
                    div.innerHTML = '<input type="radio" name="adv_probe_yn" value="' + opt.code + '" id="adv_probe_' + opt.code + '" class="adv-option-radio">' +
                        '<label for="adv_probe_' + opt.code + '" class="adv-option-label">' + (lang === 'HI' ? opt.hi : opt.en) + '</label>';
                    var radio = div.querySelector('input');
                    radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
                        yn.querySelectorAll('.adv-option-item').forEach(function(i) { i.classList.remove('selected'); });
                        div.classList.add('selected');
                        document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
                    });
                    div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') { radio.checked = true; radio.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
                    });
                    yn.appendChild(div);
                });
                answerBox.appendChild(yn);
                document.getElementById('btnAdvNext').disabled = false;
            }

            document.getElementById('btnAdvBack').style.display = 'none'; // no back during probes
        }

        function advApplySignalMap() {
            var signalMap = ADVANCED.state.signalMap || [];
            var signals   = ADVANCED.state.signals   || {};
            var applied   = 0;

            signalMap.forEach(function(m) {
                if (String(m.Is_Active || 'TRUE').toUpperCase() === 'FALSE') return;

                var sigKey  = m.Signal_Key  || m.SignalKey  || '';
                var varName = m.Lite_Variable_Name || m.LiteVariableName || '';
                var whenVal = m.When_Signal_Value  || m.WhenValue || '';
                var thenCls = m.Then_Lite_Class    || m.ThenClass || '';
                var conf    = parseFloat(m.Confidence_0_1 || m.Confidence || 0.8);

                if (!sigKey || !varName || !thenCls) return;

                var sig = signals[sigKey];
                if (sig === undefined) return;            // signal not yet derived — skip

                var sigValue = String((sig && sig.value !== undefined) ? sig.value : sig);
                if (whenVal && sigValue !== String(whenVal)) return; // value mismatch

                // Write to CPOSL.selections so Lite scoring engine can use it
                if (typeof CPOSL !== 'undefined' && CPOSL.selections) {
                    var varKey = varName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '_');
                    CPOSL.selections[varKey] = {
                        value:      thenCls,
                        auto:       true,
                        source:     'advanced_signal_map',
                        signal:     sigKey,
                        confidence: conf
                    };
                    applied++;
                }
            });

            if (applied > 0) {
                console.log('[Advanced] Signal map applied:', applied, 'Lite factor(s) populated from advanced signals');
            } else {
                console.log('[Advanced] Signal map: 0 entries applied (' + signalMap.length + ' rules, ' +
                    Object.keys(signals).length + ' signals available)');
            }
            return applied;
        }

      /**
 * Show results
 */
function advShowResults() {
  // Final inference rule pass (catches signals from the last answered question)
  advCheckInferenceDisqualifiers(null);
  // Final composite rule pass (catches signals from the last answered question)
  advApplyCompositeRules();
  // Apply signal→Lite factor mappings so scoring has full context
  advApplySignalMap();

  console.log('[Advanced] Loading results...');

  var questionCard = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
  if (questionCard) {
    questionCard.innerHTML = `
      <div style="text-align:center; padding:40px;">
        <div style="font-size:48px; margin-bottom:16px;">📊</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;">Calculating your scores...</div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
  if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';

  // ---- Build scope meta safely (ALL4 default) ----
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var scopeMeta = {
    pathwayMode: st.pathwayMode || 'ALL4',
    pathwaySet: (st.pathwaySet && st.pathwaySet.length) ? st.pathwaySet : ['AWD', 'REGEN', 'BIOCHAR', 'ERW'],
    pathwaySetKey: st.pathwaySetKey || 'ALL4',
    primaryPathway: st.pathway || 'AWD'
  };

  console.log('[Advanced][Results] Calling calculateAdvancedScores with scopeMeta:', scopeMeta);

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
      try {
        if (!result || !result.ok) {
          var err = (result && result.error) ? result.error : 'Unknown error';
          alert('Error calculating scores: ' + err);

          // Restore minimal UI instead of leaving spinner forever
          if (questionCard) {
            questionCard.innerHTML = `
              <div style="text-align:center; padding:30px;">
                <div style="font-size:42px; margin-bottom:10px;">⚠️</div>
                <div style="font-size:16px; margin-bottom:14px;">Could not calculate scores</div>
                <div style="font-size:12px; opacity:0.8; margin-bottom:18px;">${escapeHtml(err)}</div>
                <button class="btn ghost" type="button" onclick="location.reload()">Start new assessment</button>
              </div>
            `;
          }
          return;
        }

        // Useful debug: pathway coverage + includedInScope flags (from backend patch)
        if (result.pathwayScores) {
          console.log('[Advanced][Results] pathwayScores summary:',
            Object.keys(result.pathwayScores).reduce(function(acc, k){
              var p = result.pathwayScores[k] || {};
              acc[k] = {
                score: p.score,
                includedInScope: p.includedInScope,
                landCoveragePct: p.landCoveragePct,
                implCoveragePct: p.implCoveragePct,
                missingLandDims: p.missingLandDims,
                missingImplDims: p.missingImplDims
              };
              return acc;
            }, {})
          );
        }

        advRenderResults(result);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[Advanced][Results] render error:', e);
        alert('Render error: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e));
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      alert('Failed to calculate scores: ' + (error && error.message ? error.message : error));
      if (questionCard) {
        questionCard.innerHTML = `
          <div style="text-align:center; padding:30px;">
            <div style="font-size:42px; margin-bottom:10px;">⚠️</div>
            <div style="font-size:16px; margin-bottom:14px;">Backend call failed</div>
            <div style="font-size:12px; opacity:0.8; margin-bottom:18px;">${escapeHtml((error && error.message) ? error.message : String(error))}</div>
            <button class="btn ghost" type="button" onclick="location.reload()">Start new assessment</button>
          </div>
        `;
      }
    })
    .calculateAdvancedScores(st.sessionId, scopeMeta);
}

function advRenderResults(result) {
  var st = ADVANCED.state || {};
  var sigs = st.signals || {};
  var lang = (window.CPOSL && CPOSL.language) || 'EN';

  function up(x){ return String(x||'').trim().toUpperCase(); }

  function sVal(key) {
    var s = sigs[key];
    if (s == null) return null;
    if (typeof s === 'object' && s !== null && ('value' in s)) {
      var v = s.value;
      if (v == null) return null;
      return String(v).trim();
    }
    return String(s).trim();
  }
  function sConf(key) {
    var s = sigs[key];
    if (!s) return null;
    if (typeof s === 'object' && s !== null && ('confidence' in s)) return Number(s.confidence) || 0;
    return null;
  }

  // categorical -> numeric score
  function scoreFromCategorical(val) {
    var v = up(val);

    // direct numeric
    var n = parseFloat(String(val).trim());
    if (!isNaN(n)) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n));

    // readiness/eligibility patterns
    if (['READY','ELIGIBLE','PASS','ACCESSIBLE','HIGH_POTENTIAL','HIGH_POTENTIAL_CARBON_GRADE','PROGRAM_FEASIBLE','FEASIBLE','HIGH'].indexOf(v) >= 0) return 90;
    if (['PARTIAL','CONDITIONAL','PASS_WITH_FLAGS','MODERATE_SUITABILITY','MRV_READY_OR_SUPPORTABLE','SUPPORTED_FIT','MODERATE','MEDIUM','MAYBE'].indexOf(v) >= 0) return 60;
    if (['CONSTRAINED','WARNING','MIXED_BASE','LOW_PRIORITY_OR_NEEDS_PREWORK'].indexOf(v) >= 0) return 45;
    if (['NOT_READY','NOT_ELIGIBLE','FAIL','NOT_FEASIBLE','LOW_READINESS','LOW','NO','MRV_RISK'].indexOf(v) >= 0) return 15;

    // support level is inverse; higher support need => lower readiness
    if (v === 'HIGH_SUPPORT') return 30;
    if (v === 'LOW_SUPPORT') return 80;

    return 50;
  }

  // Pathway definitions (aligned to your composite/inference signals)
  var P = {
    AWD: {
      titleEN: 'AWD',
      titleHI: 'AWD',
      icon: '💧',
      relevanceKey: 'AWD_PATHWAY_RELEVANCE',
      // These are the real composite outputs you defined
      keySignals: ['AWD_TECHNICAL_READINESS', 'CARBON_ELIGIBILITY_FINAL', 'FINANCIAL_READY', 'SUPPORT_NEED_LEVEL'],
      // Prefer this as a score if present (your WEIGHTED composite can emit numeric now)
      scoreKey: 'AWD_OVERALL_READINESS'
    },
    REGEN: {
      titleEN: 'Regenerative',
      titleHI: 'रेजेन',
      icon: '🌱',
      relevanceKey: 'REGEN_PATHWAY_RELEVANCE',
      keySignals: ['REGEN_BASELINE_STATUS', 'REGEN_ADOPTION_READINESS', 'REGEN_RESOURCE_SUPPORT_FIT', 'REGEN_PROGRAM_POTENTIAL'],
      scoreKey: 'REGEN_PATHWAY_READINESS' // categorical, still scoreable
    },
    BIOCHAR: {
      titleEN: 'Biochar',
      titleHI: 'बायोचार',
      icon: '🔥',
      relevanceKey: 'BIOCHAR_PATHWAY_RELEVANCE',
      keySignals: ['BIOCHAR_FEEDSTOCK_STATUS', 'BIOCHAR_SUPPLY_PATH_READINESS', 'BIOCHAR_FIELD_APPLICATION_READINESS', 'BIOCHAR_OVERALL_READINESS'],
      scoreKey: 'BIOCHAR_OVERALL_READINESS'
    },
    ERW: {
      titleEN: 'ERW',
      titleHI: 'ERW',
      icon: '🪨',
      relevanceKey: 'ERW_PATHWAY_RELEVANCE',
      keySignals: ['ERW_MATERIAL_SUITABILITY', 'ERW_FIELD_APPLICATION_READINESS', 'ERW_SUPPLY_ACCESS_STATUS', 'ERW_OVERALL_READINESS'],
      scoreKey: 'ERW_OVERALL_READINESS'
    }
  };

  function isNotApplicable(pathKey) {
    var def = P[pathKey];
    var rel = up(sVal(def.relevanceKey));
    return (rel === 'NO' || rel === 'NOT_APPLICABLE');
  }

  function inferStatus(pathKey) {
    var def = P[pathKey];
    var rel = up(sVal(def.relevanceKey));

    if (!rel) return { labelEN:'Needs data', labelHI:'डेटा चाहिए', level:'warn' };
    if (rel === 'NO' || rel === 'NOT_APPLICABLE') return { labelEN:'Not applicable', labelHI:'लागू नहीं', level:'na' };
    if (rel === 'MAYBE' || rel === 'UNKNOWN' || rel === 'CONDITIONAL') return { labelEN:'Conditional', labelHI:'सशर्त', level:'warn' };

    // rel YES
    // Use strongest available composite signal
    for (var i=0;i<def.keySignals.length;i++){
      var v = sVal(def.keySignals[i]);
      if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== '') {
        var u = up(v);
        if (['READY','ELIGIBLE','PASS','HIGH_POTENTIAL','FEASIBLE','PROGRAM_FEASIBLE','HIGH'].indexOf(u) >= 0) return { labelEN:'Good fit', labelHI:'अच्छी फिट', level:'good' };
        if (['PARTIAL','CONDITIONAL','PASS_WITH_FLAGS','MODERATE','MEDIUM','MAYBE','SUPPORTED_FIT'].indexOf(u) >= 0) return { labelEN:'Conditional', labelHI:'सशर्त', level:'warn' };
        if (['NOT_READY','NOT_ELIGIBLE','FAIL','NOT_FEASIBLE','LOW','MRV_RISK'].indexOf(u) >= 0) return { labelEN:'Low fit', labelHI:'कम फिट', level:'bad' };
      }
    }
    return { labelEN:'Likely fit', labelHI:'संभावित', level:'good' };
  }

  function computeScore(pathKey) {
    var def = P[pathKey];

    // If backend provides per-pathway score, prefer it
    if (result && result.pathwayScores && result.pathwayScores[pathKey] && result.pathwayScores[pathKey].score != null) {
      var bs = Number(result.pathwayScores[pathKey].score);
      if (!isNaN(bs)) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(bs)));
    }

    // If not applicable, show 0 (and UI will label NA)
    if (isNotApplicable(pathKey)) return 0;

    // Prefer composite scoreKey if present
    var sk = def.scoreKey ? sVal(def.scoreKey) : null;
    if (sk != null && String(sk).trim() !== '') {
      return scoreFromCategorical(sk);
    }

    // Otherwise: average key signal scores (categorical->numeric), weighted by confidence if available
    var sum = 0, wsum = 0;
    def.keySignals.forEach(function(k){
      var v = sVal(k);
      if (v == null || String(v).trim() === '') return;
      var w = sConf(k);
      if (w == null || w <= 0) w = 0.7;
      var sc = scoreFromCategorical(v);
      sum += sc * w;
      wsum += w;
    });

    if (!wsum) return 0;
    return Math.round(sum / wsum);
  }

  function listKeySignals(pathKey) {
    var def = P[pathKey];
    // For NA pathways, don’t show "missing"
    if (isNotApplicable(pathKey)) {
      return [{ k: def.relevanceKey, v: sVal(def.relevanceKey) || 'NO' }];
    }

    var out = [];
    // Always include relevance first
    var rv = sVal(def.relevanceKey);
    if (rv != null && String(rv).trim() !== '') out.push({ k: def.relevanceKey, v: String(rv) });

    def.keySignals.forEach(function(k){
      var v = sVal(k);
      if (v == null || String(v).trim() === '') return;
      out.push({ k:k, v:String(v) });
    });

    return out.slice(0, 4);
  }

  function missingSignals(pathKey) {
    var def = P[pathKey];
    if (isNotApplicable(pathKey)) return []; // ✅ don’t show “Missing” if NA

    // Only show missing if relevance is YES/MAYBE (otherwise it’s expected)
    var rel = up(sVal(def.relevanceKey));
    if (!rel || rel === 'UNKNOWN') return [];
    if (rel === 'NO' || rel === 'NOT_APPLICABLE') return [];

    var miss = [];
    def.keySignals.forEach(function(k){
      var v = sVal(k);
      if (v == null || String(v).trim() === '') miss.push(k);
    });
    return miss.slice(0, 4);
  }

  function badgeColor(level){
    if (level === 'good') return 'rgba(16,185,129,0.20)';
    if (level === 'warn') return 'rgba(245,158,11,0.20)';
    if (level === 'na')   return 'rgba(148,163,184,0.16)'; // gray
    return 'rgba(239,68,68,0.20)';
  }
  function badgeBorder(level){
    if (level === 'good') return 'rgba(16,185,129,0.45)';
    if (level === 'warn') return 'rgba(245,158,11,0.45)';
    if (level === 'na')   return 'rgba(148,163,184,0.40)';
    return 'rgba(239,68,68,0.45)';
  }
  function barColor(level){
    if (level === 'na') return 'rgba(148,163,184,0.75)';
    return 'rgba(59,130,246,0.85)'; // keep your blue
  }

  var cards = ['AWD','REGEN','BIOCHAR','ERW'].map(function(pk){
    var def = P[pk];
    var status = inferStatus(pk);
    var score = computeScore(pk);
    var keySigs = listKeySignals(pk);
    var miss = missingSignals(pk);

    var title = (lang === 'HI') ? def.titleHI : def.titleEN;
    var statusLabel = (lang === 'HI') ? status.labelHI : status.labelEN;

    var sigHtml = keySigs.map(function(x){
      return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);">
        <div style="opacity:0.75">${escapeHtml(x.k)}</div>
        <div style="font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(String(x.v).substring(0,24))}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    var missHtml = miss.length
      ? `<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.65);">
           Missing: ${escapeHtml(miss.join(', '))}
         </div>`
      : '';

    var scoreText = (status.level === 'na') ? '—' : (score + '%');

    return `
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:240px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14);border-radius:16px;padding:14px;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;">
            <div style="font-size:22px;">${def.icon}</div>
            <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;">${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:${badgeColor(status.level)};border:1px solid ${badgeBorder(status.level)};">
            ${escapeHtml(statusLabel)}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;align-items:end;justify-content:space-between;">
          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:0.75;">Score</div>
          <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;">${scoreText}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="height:8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.10);overflow:hidden;margin-top:8px;">
          <div style="height:100%;width:${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,score))}%;background:${barColor(status.level)};"></div>
        </div>

        <div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;">
          ${sigHtml || `<div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);">No signals yet</div>`}
        </div>
        ${missHtml}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  // Optional: show any backend overall score if present
  var overallHtml = '';
  if (result && (result.overallScore != null || result.score != null)) {
    var os = (result.overallScore != null) ? Number(result.overallScore) : Number(result.score);
    if (!isNaN(os)) {
      overallHtml = `
        <div style="margin-top:14px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14);border-radius:16px;padding:14px;">
          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:0.75;">Overall score (legacy)</div>
          <div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:900;">${Math.round(os)}%</div>
        </div>
      `;
    }
  }

  var questionCard = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
  if (questionCard) {
    questionCard.innerHTML = `
      <div style="padding:18px;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;">${lang === 'HI' ? 'परिणाम' : 'Results'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:0.75;">
            Mode: <b>${escapeHtml(String(st.pathwayMode || st.pathwaySetKey || st.pathway || 'ALL4'))}</b>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:14px;display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          ${cards}
        </div>
        ${overallHtml}
        <div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <button class="btn ghost" type="button" onclick="location.reload()">Start new assessment</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
  if (answerBox) answerBox.innerHTML = '';

  var btnNext = document.getElementById('btnAdvNext');
  if (btnNext) btnNext.style.display = 'none';
  var btnBack = document.getElementById('btnAdvBack');
  if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'none';
  var btnSkip = document.getElementById('btnAdvSkip');
  if (btnSkip) btnSkip.style.display = 'none';
}

        /**
         * Show help
         */
        function showAdvancedHelp() {
            alert('Advanced Assessment:\n\n• 35-50 questions\n• 11 dimensions (D1-D6, I1-I5)\n• 25-35 minutes\n• Investment-grade report');
        }


        // Advanced module resiliency: restore missing question shell after checkpoint transitions
        function ensureAdvancedQuestionShell_() {
            var card = document.getElementById('advQuestionCard');
            var answerBox = document.getElementById('advAnswerBox');
            if (!card || !answerBox) return;

            if (!document.getElementById('advQuestionNumber')) {
                card.innerHTML =
                    '<div id="advQuestionNumber" style="display:inline-block;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);padding:6px 14px;border-radius:20px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;letter-spacing:0.5px;">Question 1 / 59</div>' +
                    '<div id="advQuestionText" style="font-size:20px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.4;margin-bottom:12px;">Loading question…</div>' +
                    '<div id="advQuestionContext" style="display:none;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);padding:12px 16px;border-radius:12px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;margin-top:12px;border-left:4px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.4);">' +
                    '<div id="advContextText"></div>' +
                    '</div>';
            }
        }

        function installAdvancedRenderGuard_() {
            if (typeof window.advRenderCurrentQuestion !== 'function' || window.__advRenderGuardInstalled) return;
            var originalRender = window.advRenderCurrentQuestion;
            window.advRenderCurrentQuestion = function() {
                ensureAdvancedQuestionShell_();
                try {
                    return originalRender.apply(this, arguments);
                } catch (err) {
                    if (err && /Cannot set properties of null/.test(String(err))) {
                        console.warn('[Advanced] Recovered missing question shell; retrying render once');
                        ensureAdvancedQuestionShell_();
                        return originalRender.apply(this, arguments);
                    }
                    throw err;
                }
            };
            window.__advRenderGuardInstalled = true;
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            var attempts = 0;
            var t = setInterval(function() {
                attempts++;
                installAdvancedRenderGuard_();
                if (window.__advRenderGuardInstalled || attempts > 40) clearInterval(t);
            }, 250);
        });
  </script>
</body>


</html>
